tamiḻ ilakkaṇap
pērakarāti




poruḷ aṇi = 1



āciriyar
paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar




tamiḻmaṇ patikkam



TamilÆ Ilakkan|ap Pe#rakara#ti
(A Tamil Grammatical Encyclopedia)






Porul@ - An|i - 1




by T.V. Gopal Iyer
Pandit of the Pondicherry Centre of the École Française d’Extrême-Orient (French School of Asian Studies)









Published by the TamilÆ Man| Pathippakam, Chenni 2005.

Pages: 32+320 = 352
Price:  330/-
     


muṉṉuri
1979ām āṇṭu navampart tiṅkaḷil tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti oṉṟaṉit tokuttu uruvākkum paṇiyil putuc cēriyil uḷḷa tolik kīḻip pirañcu ārāyccip paḷḷiyil amarttappaṭṭēṉ. iṭiyiṭiyē paṇikkappaṭṭa ēṉiya paṇi kaḷukku iṭiyilum akarātip paṇiyit toṭarntu 1995il ōraḷavu ataṉi niṟivu ceytēṉ. ip paṇiyil eṉakku utavi ceyya amarttappaṭṭa nārāyaṇacāmi aiyar, kumāracāmip piḷḷi, appācāmi mutaliyōr paṇiyiliruntu iṭiyiṭiyē viṭuvikkap paṭa ippaṇiyil eṉakku iṟutivari eṉ iḷaval kaṅkātaraṉē utavum nili ēṟpaṭṭatu. ippaṇikkut tolkāppiyattiṉ paḻiya urikaḷ mutal aṇmiyil veḷivanta pāvalarēṟu pālacuntaraṉāriṉ teṉmoḻi ilakkaṇam muṭiya uḷḷa pala nūlkaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉavāka vēṅkaṭarāculu reṭṭiyāriṉ eḻuttatikāra ārāycci, ilakkaṇak kaṭṭurikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyāriṉ eḻuttatikārac col latikārac kuṟippukkaḷ, pālacuntaraṉāriṉ tolkāppiya ārāycci kāṇṭikiyuri pōṉṟa cila nūlkaḷē mēṟkōḷkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivanta ilakkaṇam paṟṟiya kaṭṭurikaḷ pala iruppiṉum aviyellām ivvakarāti yil iṭam peṟavilli.
ivvakarāti pala tokutikaḷākap pala talippup paṟṟip pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, ellā ilakkaṇa vakikaḷukkum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷ eṅku iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpataṉi aṟitalil ciṟu cikkal ēṟpaṭum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ‘vaḻakku’ eṉṟa col tolkāppiyac ciṟappup pāyirattil mutaṟkaṇ varuvatāl accol pāyiram paṟṟiya pakutiyil viḷakkappaṭṭirukkum. talippuk kaḷiṉ akarāti amikkappaṭṭapiṉ attakiya coṟkaḷiṉ iruppiṭam aṟital eḷitākum.
ivvakarātip paṇiyil iṟuti meypput tiruttutal mutaliya vaṟṟil eṉ iḷaval kaṅkātaraṉē muḻumiyāka īṭupaṭṭa pōti lum, eṉ tampi tiruttuvataṟku muṉṉarē meyppukkaḷ tiruttattil īṭupaṭṭuc ceyaṟpaṭṭa cāṉṟōr aṉivariyum naṉṟiyoṭu nōkkukiṉṟēṉ. 17 tokutikaḷāka amiyum inta nūliṉi amippataṟku eṉakku eṉ tampi valakkiyāka utavuvatu pōlavē, innūlip patippikkum iḷavaḻakaṉārukku utaviya patippaka utaviyāḷarkaḷ celvaṉ ce. caravaṇaṉ, celvaṉ i. iṉiyaṉ, celvaṉ mu. kaliyaracaṉ, araṅka. kumarēcaṉ, vē. taṉacēkaraṉ, 
mu.nā. irāmacuppiramaṇiya irācā, nā. veṅkaṭēcaṉ, ila. tarmarācu ākiyōr innūl cemmiyāka veḷivarap peritum muyaṉṟuḷḷa ceyalip pōṟṟukiṟēṉ. ivarkaḷ nōynoṭi iṉṟip pallāṇṭu vāḻa vāḻttukiṟēṉ.
innūli veḷiyiṭa utaviya eṅkaḷ tolik kīḻip pirañcu ārāyccippaḷḷi niṟuvaṉattārukku naṉṟiyit teri vittukkoḷkiṟēṉ.
tokutikaḷ 17 : eḻuttu - 2, col - 4 , poruḷ - 11 (akam - 4, puṟam - 1, aṇi - 2, yāppu - 2, pāṭṭiyal, pāyiram, marapiyal - 1, meyppāṭu, nāṭakam, aḷavi, niyāyam ēṉiya - 1)

ti.vē. kōpāliyar


mukavuri
eḻuttatikāra ilakkaṇap pērakarātiyil, tolkāppiyam mutalāka iṉṟu nammiṭiyē vāḻntu varum ilakkaṇa ilakkiya moḻiyiyal pēraṟiñarām ca.pālacuntaraṉār ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil iyaṟṟiya  ‘teṉṉūl’ muṭiya, iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭuvaṉavāka nilavi varum ciṟanta ilakkaṇa nūl variciyil iṭampeṟum nūlkaḷum urikaḷum iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ikkālattil tolkāppiyak kaṭal eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭum annūl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kālattē atu ciṟunūlākavē yākkap peṟṟatu. ataṉ eḻuttup paṭalattil uḷḷa 9 iyalkaḷilum vitik kappaṭuvaṉa ellām karuviyum ceykiyum eṉa iruvakippaṭum.  avaṟṟuḷ karuvi nūlmarapu mutaliya nāṉku ōttum, ceyki tokimarapu mutaliya eñciya aintu ōttum ām.  karuvitāṉum potuvum ciṟappum eṉa iruvakittu. mutal mūṉṟu ōttum potukkaruvi; ceyki oṉṟaṟkēyuriya puṇariyal ciṟappuk karuvi.
nūlmarapu, nūliṉatu marapu paṟṟiya peyarkaḷākiya eḻuttu - kuṟil - neṭil - uyir - mey - meyyiṉ vakikaḷ - eḻuttukkaḷiṉ māttiri - iṉṉa meykku iṉṉamey naṭpeḻuttu, paki yeḻuttu eṉpataṉik kuṟikkum meymmayakkam - meyyeḻut tukkaḷ, ekara okara uyirkaḷ, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṉu mivi puḷḷipeṟutal - makarak kuṟukkam uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyum kōṭal - cuṭṭu - viṉā - aḷapeṭi - eṉpaṉa paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.
aṭuttu, moḻimarapu kūṟum vitikaḷ nūlmarapiṉ oḻipāka amintuḷḷaṉa. itaṉkaṇ kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam, aḷapeṭi, moḻiyākkam, īroṟṟuṭaṉili, makarak kuṟukkam, aikārak kuṟukkam, pōliyeḻuttukkaḷ, moḻi mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷ, moḻiyīṟṟil varum eḻuttukkaḷ - eṉpaṉa iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
piṟappiyal, uyir - mey - cārpeḻuttukkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ piṟappiṭaṅkaḷum muyaṟciyum paṟṟi moḻikiṟatu.
puṇariyalil, ellāmoḻikaḷiṉ iṟutiyum mutalum mey uyir eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum; peyarum toḻilum eṉṟō toḻilum peyarum eṉṟō perumpāṉmiyum coṟkaḷ puṇarumiṭattu iyalpākavum tirintum puṇarum; puṇarvaṉa nilimoḻiyiṉ īṟṟeḻuttum varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttum ākiya iraṇṭumē; coṟkaḷ vēṟṟumipporuḷ paṟṟiyum al vaḻipporuḷ paṟṟiyum puṇarum; iṭiyē cāriyi varappeṟum; cila cāriyikaḷ uruttirintum puṇarum; eḻuttuccāriyikaḷ ivi, uṭampaṭumey ivi - eṉa icceytikaḷik kāṇalām.
tokimarapu eṉṉum aintām ōttiṉkaṇ, uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷḷum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷḷum īṟukaḷtōṟum virittu muṭippaṉavaṟṟi orōvoru cūttirat tāl tokuttu muṭipu kūṟappaṭuvaṉavum, uyartiṇippeyarp puṇarcciyum viravuppeyarp puṇarcciyum iraṇṭām mūṉṟām vēṟṟumiyurupu ēṟṟa peyarppuṇarcciyum, cila iṭiccoṟ kaḷatu muṭipum, eṇ niṟi aḷavup peyaruḷ cilavaṟṟatu puṇarcci yum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
urupiyal, urupēṟṟa peyar cāriyi peṟṟum peṟāmalum orōvaḻi neṭumutal kuṟukiyum varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. uyirmayaṅkiyalilum puḷḷimayaṅkiyalilum alvaḻip puṇarcci perumpālum eḻuvāyttoṭarkkē koḷḷappaṭu kiṟatu.  vēṟṟumippuṇarcci vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ tokka vēṟ ṟumip puṇarccikkē koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.  ippuṇarccikaḷ potuvākak kūṟappaṭṭālum, āciriyar uyartiṇippeyar - viravuppeyar - kiḷippeyar - nāṭpeyar - tiṅkaṭ peyar - eṇ niṟi aḷavup peyar - eṉpaṉavaṟṟi vitantōtiyē muṭikkum karuttiṉarātaliṉ, ippuṇarccikaḷ aḵṟiṇiyil, kiḷi mutalākac collappaṭṭa appeyarkaḷ nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiyavaṟ ṟiṟkē kōṭal āciriyar karuttām.
āciriyar īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉṟu kūṟiyami īreḻuttuk kuṟṟukarac collit tam maṉattuk koṇṭamiyālām.  kuṟṟiya lukaram puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkum. uyiraḷa peṭi eṉpatu neṭili aṭuttu varum otta iṉakkuṟṟeḻuttē.  puṇarcciyil tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟippiṭum eḻuttuppēṟaḷa peṭi piṟkālattē vaḻakku iṟantatu eṉṟu kōṭal takum.
tolkāppiyatti aṭuttu nāṭṭil ciṟappāka vaḻaṅki vantatākak karutappaṭum avinayam irācapavittirap pallavatariyaṉ uriyoṭu 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari vaḻakkiliruntu vanta ceyti naṉṉūl mayilinātar uriyālēyē peṟappaṭukiṟatu.  annūlum ataṉuriyum vaḻakkiṟantu viṭṭaṉa.
tolkāppiyatti aṭuttu iṉṟu vaḻakkil irukkum aintilakkaṇa nūl peruntēvaṉār uriyoṭu kūṭiya vīra cōḻiyamē. pallavar kālattilē “pāratatēyattu vaḻakkilirukkum ellā moḻikaḷukkum tāy camaṟkirutamē” eṉṟa karuttu makka ḷiṭiyē uruvāka, atu 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭu muṭiya uṟutiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭu vantatu.  11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil tōṉṟiya vīra cōḻiyam ikkaruttiyuṭkoṇṭu iyaṟṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamē.
vīracōḻiyattiṉkaṇ, uyireḻuttukkaḷi aṭuttu mey yeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṉṉar neṭuṅkaṇakkil āytam iṭam peṟṟa ceyti kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  makarak kuṟukkam mēlē peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷēyum oru puḷḷi peṟum eṉṟa nuṭpamāṉa ceyti innūliṉ uriyiṉkaṇṇēyē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  vaṭamoḻip puṇarcciyil alvaḻi, vēṟṟumi eṉṟa poruḷ paṟṟiya pākupāṭu illi.  ākavē, vīracōḻiyac cantip paṭalattilum alvaḻi vēṟṟumip pākupāṭu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭilatu.  varumoḻi mutalil uyirvariṉ kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭum eṉṉum ceyti innūlilēyē mutaṟkaṇ kuṟippiṭappaṭa, acceyti nēminātam naṉṉūl mutaliya piṉ ṉūlkaḷ palavaṟṟilum iṭampeṟalāyiṟṟu.  vaṭamoḻiccoṟkaḷ tamiḻolikkēṟpat tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭumāṟṟiṟku innūl kūṟum vitikaḷē naṉṉūl mutaliya piṉṉūlkaḷilum koḷḷappaṭalāyiṉa.  vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa ‘na’ eṉṟa etirmaṟi muṉoṭṭu varumoḻi yōṭu iṇiyumiṭattu ēṟpaṭum tiripukaḷi innūl iyampiṭa, ataṉi nēminātamum ēṟṟukkoḷḷa, naṉṉūl ataṉi nekiḻttu viṭṭatu, ḷakarattiṟkuk kūṟum puṇarcciviti ḻakarattiṟkum, in nūlāciriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil piṉpaṟṟappaṭavē, in nūlāciriyar ilakkiyam kaṇṭu ataṟku ilakkaṇam varintuḷḷa ceyti innūlil putitāka iṭam peṟukiṟatu.  icceytiyi nēminātam naṉṉūl pōṉṟa piṉṉūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭavilli.  uṭampaṭumeyyi innūlāciriyar ‘i ī ai vaḻi yavvum, ēṉi uyirvaḻi vavvum, ē muṉ ivvirumiyum, eṉṟu mutaṉmuṟiyāka variyaṟuttuk kūṟiyavarāvar. “aḷapeṭi mūṉṟu māttiri peṟum; atu neṭilum kuṟilum iṇin tolikkum ōroliyē” eṉṟa ivaratu koḷkiyē, perumpālum piṉṉūlār palarālum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. ē yā e - eṉpaṉa col mutalil viṉāvākum eṉṟa ivar karuttip piṟkālattu nūlār palarum ēṟṟukkoṇṭavarāvar.
aṭuttu vanta nēminātamum, neṭuṅkaṇakku variciyi , uyir - aṭuttu āytam - aṭuttu mey - eṉṟē kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. in nūluḷ kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, aikārak kuṟukkam eṉṉum ivaṟṟōṭu oḷakārak kuṟukkamum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. “uyiraḷa peṭi neṭiloṭu kūṭiya ōroliyām iṉak kuṟṟeḻuttu; atu mūṉṟu māttiri peṟum” eṉṟu vīracōḻiyatti oṭṭi nēminātam nuvalkiṟatu.  vaṭamoḻit tattitānta nāmaṅkaḷum etir maṟi yuṇarttum nakara muṉṉoṭṭup puṇarcciyum vīracōḻi yattip piṉpaṟṟiyē kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  vīracōḻiyam vitikkum vaṭamoḻiyākkam nēminātattil illi.
tolkāppiyatti aṭuttu makkaḷ uḷḷattē ciṟappāka iṭampeṟuvatu pavaṇanti muṉivar iyaṟṟiya naṉṉūlē.  muta leḻuttum cārpeḻuttum eṉṟa pākupāṭu - cārpeḻuttup pattu eṉpatu - avi ovvoṉṟum paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ - moḻikku mutalil, iṭiyil, īṟṟil varum eḻuttukkaḷ - pōliyeḻut tukkaḷ - eṉpaṉa eḻuttiyalil iṭampeṟṟuḷa.  uyiraḷapeṭi neṭṭeḻuttiṉ nīṭṭamākiya mūṉṟu māttiri, kuṟil aṟikuṟi yāka varuvatē eṉṟa ceyti kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.  pataviyalil, pakupatam pakuti - vikuti  mutaliya uṟuppuk kaḷākap pirittuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.  pakupata uṟuppukkaḷ viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pataviyal naṉṉūl kuṟippiṭum orumoḻip puṇarcciyākiya putuc ceytiyē. itaṉ iṟutiyil vaṭamoḻi ākkam vīracōḻiyattip piṉpaṟṟi variyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ‘na’ eṉṟa etirmaṟi muṉṉoṭṭup paṟṟiya ceyti pēcappaṭṭilatu.
tolkāppiyattiṟku iḷampūraṇar varinta uriyiyum uṭkoṇṭu iyaṟṟappeṟṟa naṉṉūlil tolkāppiyac ceytikaḷ palavaṟṟoṭum avvuriyāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭa ceytikaḷum iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyam kuṟikkum puṇariyal - toki marapu - uyirmayaṅkiyal - kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal - paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ palavum uyirīṟṟup puṇariyaluḷ aṭakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  alvaḻi yāvaṉa iviyeṉa viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu. uṭampaṭumey, kuṟṟukaram uyirvarak keṭutal - pōṉṟavi vīracōḻiyattip piṉpaṟṟiyaṉavām.  naṉṉūlil kāṇappaṭum māṟṟaṅkaḷ ‘marapu nili tiriyātu’ amintaṉa eṉpa.  tolkāppiyattiṉiṉṟu naṉṉūl caṟṟē vēṟupaṭṭuk kūṟumiṭaṅkaḷ poruḷnili tiriyāmiyāl ‘marapu nili tiriyā māṭcimi’ yuṭiyavāy mutalnūṟku malivupaṭāmal cellum iyaṟkiya ātalic civañāṉamuṉivar tamatu pāyiraviruttiyuḷ viḷaṅkak kūṟumāṟu īṇṭuk karutal takum.
ilakkaṇaviḷakkam, naṉṉūl tolkāppiyattoṭu māṟu paṭṭuk kūṟum oru cila iṭaṅkaḷic cuṭṭi viḷakkukiṟatu. taṉakku muṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷil viḷakkappaṭum vaṭamoḻiyākkatti iv vilakkaṇanūl nekiḻttuviṭṭatu.
ilakkaṇakkottu, pirayōka vivēkam eṉpaṉavaṟṟil cila ariya puṇarccivitikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. a - an - na - ni - ku - vi - puṇarap puṇarppatu vaṭamoḻiyil etirmaṟiyākum eṉṟa ceyti ilakkaṇakkottil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻc ciṟap peḻuttu aintāṉum tirintu āriyaccol vaṭacollākit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ceyti ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷilum viḷakkappaṭu kiṟatu. icceyti naṉṉūlil iṭam peṟṟilatu.
toṉṉūl viḷakkam naṉṉūlip peritum piṉpaṟṟiyatu; vaṭamoḻiyākkattilum naṉṉūlip piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷatu. muttu vīriyattil tīrkkacanti mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku vitikaḷ taṉittaṉiyē kūṟap paṭṭuḷa. kō + il = kōyil, mā + iru = māyiru - mutaliya vaṟṟiṟkut taṉiyē vitikaḷ vakukkappaṭṭuḷa.  cuvāminātattil kuṟippiṭattakum vicēṭamāka ētum iṉṟu. ataṉ āciriya virutta yāppu nayaṉuṟumāṟu illi.
tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattu iḷampūraṇar uri - nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri - ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu moḻiyiyal vittakarām vēṅkaṭarāculu reṭṭiyār, pi.cā. cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār ivarkaḷtam ārāyccik kuṟippukkaḷ - ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutiyil veḷivanta pālacuntaraṉārtam tolkāppiyak kāṇṭikiyuri - civañāṉa muṉivar aracañcaṇmukaṉār varin tuḷḷa viruttiyurik kuṟippukkaḷ eṉṉumiviyum, vīracōḻiyam peruntēvaṉār uri - nēminātam vayiramēkaviruttiyuri - naṉṉūl mayilinātar, caṅkaranamaccivāyar,  irāmānucak kavirāyar, caṭakōparāmānucāccāriyār āṟumukanāvalar eṉṟiṉṉōrtam urikaḷ - eṉṉum iviyum ēṉiya mūla nūlkaḷiṉ ceyti kaḷoṭu tokukkappaṭṭu ivveḻuttatikāra ilakkaṇap pērakarāti uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itaṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum kuṟṟam kuṟikaḷi nallaṟiñar perumakkaḷ uriya kāraṇam cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvārāyiṉ, appiḻi pāṭukaḷ aṭutta patippil kaḷiyappaṭum. aṉṉa tiruttaṅkaḷ naṉṟi yaṟitalōṭu ēṟkappaṭum.

ti.vē. kōpāliyar

Eva Wilden
École Française d’Extrême-Orient	16&19 Dumas Street,
Pondicherry centre	Pondicherry.
Aug. 2005
Introduction
Tamil grammar and poetics are old and venerable disciplines interwoven into a complex system the beginnings of which are lost in legend. What is fact, however, is that we are looking back on a textual tradition representing the thought of almost two millennia: a continuous discourse on Tamil language and literature, but also a dispute with other systems of knowledge, most notably the Sanskrit grammatical and poetological traditions. To give a rough chronology, for the first millennium, we have one great treatise encompassing the whole field and developing the basic structure that is taken up, with some modifications and extensions, by the whole later tradition. This is the famous Tolka#ppiyam, consisting of three parts, two of them devoted to two different aspects of grammar, which has been ever since split into two sections, namely ElÈuttu (phonology) and Col (morphology and syntax), while the third part treats of Porul@ (poetics). It is followed by a small work specialising in a particular field of poetics, namely that of Akam (love poetry), called the IrÈaiyan_a#r Akapporul@. 
The second millennium, probably a time of socio-cultural upheaval, sets in with a voluminous commentary tradition not only for the treatises that had been written so far, but also for wide parts of the older literature. In fact our understanding of the meaning of the older texts is basically indebted to these commentaries. Nevertheless, there is a parallel development of new treatises in all sub-disciplines, mirroring the confrontation with the change of language, the arising of new literary forms and the massive impact of North-Indian, i.e. Sanskritic modes of thinking and writing in the Tamilian South. To mention just a few of the most important titles, among the inclusive texts – comprising, just as the Tolka#ppiyam, the whole range of the field – there are the heavily Sanskritised Vi#raco#lÈiyam of the 11th century, and the Tamil-conservative Ilakkan|a Vil@akkam of the 17th century. Both of them extend the original structure of three sections, dealing with ElÈuttu (phonology), Col (morphology and syntax), and Porul@ (poetics), by another two subsumed under Porul@, namely Ya#ppu (metrics) and An|i (figures of speech). Among the influential treatises devoted exclusively to grammar we may list the Nan_n_u#l (12th century), the standard book on Tamil grammar after the Tolka#ppiyam, and the Pirayo#ka Vive#kam (17th century), agin very Sanskritic. Poetics, for its part, seems to have been an even more fruitful domin, creating a number of branches with various specia-lisations. The first independent text on metrics is the Ya#pparun)-kalakka#riki (10th century); the most notable exponent of systematic Akam poetics is the Nampi Akapporul@ (12th century), while the Pur_am genre (heroic poetry) is represented by the Pur_apporul@ven|pa#-ma#li (9th century).
The encyclopedia presented here is an attempt to render accessible this wealth of materials to specialists and also to non-specialists. The vast topology and terminology of Tamil grammar and poetics are represented by key terms which are explined with reference to the corresponding su#tras in the treatises and additional explication from the various commentaries. The whole work comprises 17 volumes, structured in the traditional way into the three sections ElÈuttu (phonology), Col (morphology and syntax) and Porul@ (poetics), where 2 volumes fall on ElÈuttu, 4 on Col, and 11 on the various sub-disciplines subsumed under poetics: 2 for Ya#ppu (metrics), 2 for An|i (figures of speech), 4 for Akam (love topics), 1 for Pur_am (heroic topics), 1 for Pa#t@t@iyal (literary genres), Pa#yiram (prefaces) and Marapiyal (word usage), and finally 1 for Meyppa#t@u (physical manifestation), Na#t@akam (drama), Al@avi (valid means of knowledge), A#nantakkur|r|am (collocations to be avoided), Niya#yam (logic) and ValÈuvamiti (poetic licence). The last of these volumes contins a bibliography.
This sort of work of synthesis has long been a desideratum of research: it gives erudite references to a vast range of technical Tamil texts which are, for the most part, not well understood today. Some of the texts are hard to come by – unless in the editions of the author of this encyclopedia (on whom more below) –  most of them are not translated into any other language, general introductions into the field are few, and even fewer are written in languages more easily accessible to the general reader (like English or French). There has been more than one project comparable in range in recent years, most prominently the Encyclopedia of Tamil literature of the Institute of Asian Studies, Chenni (in English language), but sadly this opus has not yet seen more than 3 volumes, the last one already nine years old and reaching only the letter “ai”. All the more reason for scholars interested in Tamil language and literature to be grateful to the author of the present work, the venerable T.V. Gopal Iyer, with his 80 years one of the last living exponents of a great tradition of exegesis.
Space permits here no more than a brief account of the highlights of a long and in many respects exemplary career of a Tamil savant in the 20th century. As well as following a traditional path of education, the worldly marks of which are his two titles Vidvan and Panditam conferred by the University of Madras and the Maturi Tamil Cankam respectively (in 1945 and 1953), Gopal Iyer also acquired the “modern” university degrees of Bachelor of Oriental Language and Bachelor of Oriental Language with Honours at the University of Madras (1951 and 1958). From 1965-1978 he tught in Rajah’s College, Thiruviyaru, in which period he already took up his activity of editing works of Classical Tamil, especially theoretical texts. The most important publications from that phase are the Ilakkan|a Vil@akkam in 8 volumes (published in Thanjavur by the Sarasvati Mahal from 1971-1974), the Ilakkan|ak Kottu (Sarasvati Mahal 1973) and the Pirayoka Vivekam (Sarasvati Mahal 1973).
Ever since 1978, Mr. Gopal Iyer’s sphere of activity has shifted to Pondicherry, where he has been (and still is) employed as a research scholar by the École Française d’Extrême-Orient (EFEO) – i.e., the French School of Asian Studies –, a research institution financed by the French government which has 17 research centres spread across Asia, the westernmost of which is that in Pondicherry, and which has the mission of studying Asian (and notably Indian) languages, cultures and religions. In this environment, designed as a meeting place for international research, the enormous preparatory work for this encyclopedia has been accomplished. Part of the voluminous editorial output of Mr. Gopal Iyer during the last 27 years has appeared in a series co-published by the EFEO and the IFP (the French Institute of Pondicherry, another research Institution of the French government in whose premises T.V. Gopal Iyer worked for several years), such as a 3-volume edition of the Te#va#ram, his major contribution to devotional Tamil literature (1984f., 1991), and the Ma#ran_ Akapporul@ (2005). A number of further publications deserve mention, since they concern fundamental texts of the Tamil grammatical and poetological tradition upon which the encyclopedia is based. Last year he published a 14-volume edition of the complete Tolka#ppiyam with all the commentaries (through Thiru. G. Ela-vazhagan of TamizhMann Pathippagam, Chenni - 17) and this year editions of the Vi#raco#l@iyam and the Ma#r_an_ Alan)ka#ram.
The EFEO is extremely happy that it has been able to contribute its share to this publication of vital importance for the exploration of Tamil literary history, which will be a monument to a most extrordinary man, who has been teacher and adviser, nay, a living encyclopedia, to so many students of Tamil language, Indian and Western.

A final brief note of thanks to three individuals who were important in bringing this large work to the light of day. The first is Mr. T.V. Gopal Iyer’s younger brother, Mr. T.S. Gangadharan, then of the French Institute of Pondicherry and now of the Pondi-cherry Centre of the EFEO, who wrote the work out in a fair hand.  The second is Dr. Jean-Luc Chevillard, who, years later, had the encyclopeadia digitally photographed when in its yet more voluminous hand-written state and so by his timely intervention prevented the loss of some of its parts.  The third is the publisher, Mr. Ela-vazhagan, of the Thamizh Mann Pathippagam, who had the vision to see the value of this work and took on the task of setting it in type, a labour which took a year and a half and involved five sets of proofs.


īvā vilṭaṉ
pirañcu intiya āyvup paḷḷi				16&19  ṭumās teru
putuccēri miyam							pāṇṭiccēri,
ākasṭu 2005.
aṟimukavuri
tamiḻ ilakkaṇam miku toṉmi vāyntatu; peru matippiṟ kuriyatu; taṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷ piṇintu nuṭpamāṉa pēramippāka uruvākiyuḷḷa ivaṟṟiṉ toṭakkak kālam eḷitil variyaṟukka muṭiyāta paḻaṅkālamākum. tamiḻ moḻi paṟṟiyum ilakkiyam paṟṟiyum 2000 āṇṭukaḷāka iṭiyaṟātu toṭarntu vantuḷḷa cintaṉikaḷi nām iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa nūlkaḷiliruntu kāṇkiṟōm. accintaṉikaḷ piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ (kuṟippāka vaṭa moḻiyiṉ) ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷōṭu uṟaḻntu vantuḷḷati yum kāṇkiṟōm. tōrāyamākac coṉṉāl mutal āyiram āṇṭukaḷil tamiḻilakkiyattiṟku muḻumiyāṉa aṭippaṭiyāka amintuḷḷa orē perum pērilakkaṇam tolkāppiyam ākum. (aḵtu avvappoḻutu ciṟcila māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉum virivākkaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum avvāyiram āṇṭukkāla ilakkiyattukkumē aṭip paṭiyāka amintatu.) anta orē ilakkaṇamtāṉ pukaḻmiku tolkāppiyam. aḵtu mūṉṟu atikāraṅkaḷ koṇṭatu: eḻuttu (ñāṭinṭiṭaṭipala) col (āṭicayīhṭiṭaṭipala yaṉē ḷulavēyo) poruḷ (ñaṭinaviuḷa). cila kālam kaḻittup poruḷilakkaṇattiṉ oru pakutiyāṉa akam paṟṟi iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ eṉṉum ciṟunūl oṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu.
ki.pi. 1000-kkup piṉṉar tamiḻakattil camutāya - kalācāra māṟṟaṅkaḷ virivupeṟṟaṉa. akkālakaṭṭattil tōṉṟiyatē virivāka uriyeḻutum muṟiyākum. ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukku maṭṭu maṉṟi, paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil perumpālaṉavaṟṟukkum ivvāṟu urikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. appaḻanūlkaḷiṉ poruḷit terintukoḷḷap perumaḷavukku avvurikaḷiyē nām cārn tuḷḷōm. eṉiṉum akkālakaṭṭattil tamiḻ ilakkaṇattiṉ uṭpirivukaḷ paṟṟip putu nūlkaḷum tōṉṟalāyiṉa. moḻiyil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟṟaṅkaḷ, putiya ceyyuḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, tamiḻiṉmītu vaṭanāṭṭu aḵtāvatu camaṟkirutam cārnta cintaṉi eḻuttu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ tākkam ivaṟṟik kāṭṭuvaṉavāka apputu nūlkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. mutaṉmiyāṉa cilavaṟṟik kāṇpōm. tol kāppiyam pōl eḻuttu, col, poruḷ mūṉṟiyum paṟṟi eḻutappaṭṭavi vīracōḻiyamum (mikunta camaṟkirutac cārpuṭiyatu; 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) ilakkaṇa viḷakkamum (tamiḻ ilakkaṇat tol marapukaḷik kaṭippiṭittatu; 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) ākum. innūlkaḷ poruḷi yāppu (ānavaciuḷa) aṇi (kupiracanaḷa ṭika ḷuyīnanuh) eṉṟu mēlum iru pirivukaḷāka ākkiyuḷḷaṉa. eḻuttukkum collukkum maṭṭum ilakkaṇam vakuttaṉavaṟṟuḷ mikutiyum payilappaṭṭavi 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu naṉṉūlum (tolkāppiyattukkup piṉ tamiḻukku ilakkaṇam eṉṟālē naṉṉūl tāṉ eṉpatē nilimi) 17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭup pirayōka vivēkamum (mikutiyum camaṟkirutac cārpuṭiyatu) ākum. poruḷiṉ pirivukaḷ paṟṟiyum uṭ pirivukaḷ paṟṟiyum eḻunta putu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ mikappala. yāppu paṟṟit taṉiyāka eḻunta mutal ilakkaṇam yāpparuṅkalak kāriki (10ām nūṟṟāṇṭu); akam paṟṟi virivāka eḻutappaṭṭa kuṟippiṭattakka nūl nampi akapporuḷ (12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu); puṟam paṟṟiyatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli (9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu).
inta tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti mēṟcoṉṉa ilakkaṇac celvaṅkaḷit tamiḻ vallunarkaḷukkum piṟarukkum - oru cērat tokuttut tarum ciṟanta nūl. tamiḻ ilak kaṇattiṉ (poruḷiyal uṭpaṭa) mika virinta parappil kaṇṭa aṉittu mukkiyamāṉa talippu ovvoṉṟum akara varicippaṭi, uriya nūṟpākkaḷum, palvēṟu urikārarkaḷ kūṟṟukkaḷum tarappaṭṭut teḷivāka viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu. eḻuttukku 2, collukku 4, poruḷukku 11 āka 17 tokutikaḷ koṇṭatu iv vakarāti. (poruḷ paṟṟiya 11 tokutikaḷiṉ vakippāṭu: akam - 4, puṟam - 1, yāppu - 2; aṇi - 2; pāṭṭiyal, pāyiram, marapiyal - 1; meyppāṭu, nāṭakam, aḷavi, āṉantak kuṟṟam, niyāyam, vaḻuvamiti ākiyavi - 1, eṉpaṉavākum) iṟutit tokutil karuvi nūṟpaṭṭiyalum uḷḷatu.
poruḷkaḷit teḷḷitiṉ uṇarntu muṟippaṭi viḷakkum ittakiya pērakarātiyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāt tēvi neṭunāḷāka āyvulakattāl uṇarappaṭṭuvantatākum. mika virinta iv vilak kaṇanūlkaḷum urikaḷum tiṭpa nuṭpamāṉa naṭiyilamintavi yākiyāl iṉṟu eḷitil paṭittuṇarattakkavi alla. avaṟṟil kāṇattakum ilakkaṇac celvaṅkaḷuk kellām pulami cāṉṟa viḷakkaṅkaḷi iṅkuk kāṇalām.
ivvilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ, urikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiṉ accuppaṭikaḷ kūṭa eḷitil kiṭṭuvatilli (kiṭṭiṉum aviyum ivvakarāti yāciriyar acciṭṭaviyākavē irukkum; avarip paṟṟi mēlum cila piṉṉar). avvariya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ piṟamoḻikaḷil peyarkkappaṭātavi; ivaṟṟip paṟṟiya potuvāṉa viḷakka nūlkaḷum cilavē - atuvum āṅkilam, pireñcu pōṉṟa moḻi kaḷil vantuḷḷavi oṉṟiraṇṭēyākum. aṇmik kālaṅkaḷil ivi pōṉṟa virivāṉa nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭum tiṭṭaṅkaḷ cila vaṟṟuḷ ceṉṉi āciyaviyal niṟuvaṉam āṅkilattil veḷiyiṭat toṭaṅkiya “tamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḷañciyamum” oṉṟu. akkaḷañci yattil mūṉṟu maṭalaṅkaḷē (ai muṭiya) veḷivanta niliyil, kaṭanta oṉpatāṇṭukaḷākap paṇi muṭṭuppaṭṭu niṟkiṟatu eṉpatu niṉikkattakkatu. inniliyil tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷilum urikaḷilum ūṟiya pēraṟiñarkaḷil iṉṟu nammōṭu uḷḷa mikac cilaril oruvarāṉa, 80 vayatu niṟinta vaṇakkattukkuriya 
ti.vē. kōpāliyariṉ paṭippāṉa ip pērakarāti tamiḻ ilakkaṇa, ilakkiya aṟiñarkaḷ avarpāl naṉṟi pārāṭṭutaṟkuriya oṉṟākum.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷil palvakiyilum pōṟṟattakkavaruḷ oruvarāṉa ivvāciriyariṉ neṭiya tamiḻppaṇi kuṟittu mikac ciṟiya aḷavilēyē īṇṭuk kūṟa iyalum. pāram-pariyamāṉa tamiḻp pulami marapil avar peṟṟa takutikaḷ ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka vittuvāṉ (1945); maturit tamiḻc caṅkap paṇṭitam (1953) ākiyavi; attōṭu “navīṉa”p palkalik kaḻakap paṭṭaṅ kaḷākac ceṉṉip palkali kaḻakattil 1951il pi.ō.el paṭṭamum, 1958il pi.ō.el (āṉarcu) paṭṭamum peṟṟuḷḷār. 1965 - 1978il avar tiruviyāṟu aracar kallūriyil āciriyappaṇi āṟṟiṉār. appoḻutē patippup paṇiyi, kuṟippāka paḻantamiḻ ilakkaṇa urinūlkaḷip patippikkum paṇiyit toṭaṅkiṉār. akkāla kaṭṭattil avar patippittavi ilakkaṇa viḷakkam 8 tokutikaḷ (tañci caracuvati makāl 1971-74), ilakkaṇak kottu (tañci caracuvati makāl 1973), pirayōka vivēkam (tañci caracuvati makāl 1973) ākiyaviyām.
1978iliruntu tiru kōpāliyar avarkaḷuṭiya aṟi vāṟṟalip pireñcu intiya āyvup paḷḷi (viuṭiṭana kucayanḷīyaḷina ṉa’nuovacaṣīana-ṭīcanivē ) payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟatu. avar iṉṟu āyvup paṇi ceyyum anniṟuvaṉam pirāṉcu nāṭṭu aracu niti utaviyuṭaṉ naṭipeṟukiṟatu. av vamippiṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil mottam 17 āyvumiyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. pāṇṭiccēri miyam uṭpaṭa. ivi āciya (kuṟippāka) intiya moḻikaḷ, paṇpāṭukaḷ, camayaṅkaḷ ākiyavi kuṟittu āyvu ceykiṉṟaṉa. paṉṉāṭṭu āyvāḷarkaḷ oruṅkiṇintu aṟivup paṇi ceyyum ic cūḻal koṇṭa pāṇṭiccēri miyattiltāṉ ivvilakkaṇak kaḷañciyam tokukkum māperum paṇi naṭantatu. kaṭanta 27 āṇṭukaḷil tiru kōpāliyar paṭitta paṟpala nūlkaḷiyum pāṇṭiccēriyi luḷḷa pireñcu intiya āyvup paḷḷiyum, pirāṉcu nāṭṭu araciṉ maṟṟoru kīḻik kali āyvu niṟuvaṉamāṉa pireñcu niṟuvaṉamum (kucananuh aiḷēvaviravana) veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvāṟu veḷiyāṉa avar nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavi tamiḻp pakti ilakkiyam cārnta tēvāram (3 tokutikaḷ 1984 mutal 1991 vari), māṟaṉ akap poruḷ (2005) ākiyavi. ippērakarātikku aṭippaṭiyāka amintaviyum kōpāliyar patippittaviyumāṉa vēṟu cila nūlkaḷiyum kuṟippiṭṭākavēṇṭum; avi (tiru. kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam, ceṉṉi - 17 mūlamāka) avar 2004il patippitta tolkāppiyamum (urikaḷuṭaṉ 14 tokutikaḷ) 2005il avar patippittuḷḷa vīracōḻiyamum māṟaṉ alaṅkāramum ākum.
tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇa varalāṟṟi āyvu ceytiṭa iṉṟi yamiyāta karuvi ip pērakarāti. intiya maṟṟum mēlnāṭṭut tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷ palarukku ācāṉākavum aṟivuriyāḷa rākavum, ēṉ naṭamāṭum kalikkaḷañciyamākavum aminta apūrva māṉa ōr aṟiñariṉ māperuñ cātaṉiyāka amivatum itu. eṉavē innūl veḷiyīṭṭil tāṉum paṅku peṟṟu utaviṭa vāyppup peṟṟatu kuṟittup pireñcu intiya āyvup paḷḷi makiḻkiṟatu.

iṟutiyāka immāperum paṇi veṟṟikaramāka naṭipeṟap perumpaṅku āṟṟiya mūvarukku naṉṟi kūṟiyāka vēṇṭum. mutalā mavar tiru kōpāliyariṉ iḷaval tiru kaṅkātaraṉ avarkaḷ. avar muṉṉarp pireñcu niṟuvaṉattil paṇi ceytavar. ippoḻutu pi.i.ā.pa. pāṇṭiccēri miyattil avar āyvaṟiñar, paṇiyil uḷḷār. nūl muḻuvatiyum tam kippaṭa aḻakāka eḻutiyavar avar. iraṇṭāmavar ṭākṭar ḻāṉ-luk-ceviyar; kiyeḻuttup pirati muḻuvatiyum ṭijiṭal niḻaṟpaṭamāka eṭuttu eppakutiyum citilamāki aḻintu viṭātapaṭi pārttuk koṇṭavar. mūṉṟāmavar tiru kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ. avar innūliṉ ciṟappiyum payaṉiyum uṇarntu ati veḷiyiṭa muṉvantavar. ip perunūlic cemmi yākak kaṇiṉiyil taṭṭaccu ceytu accukku aṇiyamākkavum, meyppukkaḷi aintu muṟi kavaṉamākat tiruttavum āka oṉṟari āṇṭukaḷ avarum avaric cārntavarkaḷum uḻittuḷḷaṉar.


tamiḻ vāḻka!
talimāmaṇiyeṉat tamiḻilakkaṇap pērakarātiyi
varintaruḷiya kalimāmaṇi,
paṇṭitavittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar
avarkaḷi vāḻtti vaḻaṅkum
“pārāṭṭuri”
1	aṉpum aruḷum aṟivum tiruvum
	iṉpamum eḻilum maṉṉiya uruvāyc
	colloṭu poruḷpōl ellum oḷiyum
	pulliya vaṭiveṉa ammi yappaṉāyc
5	centamiḻ mayamāyt tikaḻum cīrcāl
	poṉmali mēviya puricaṭik kaṭavuḷ
	nāṉmaṟi navilum nayaṉmiku nāvāl
	cinti cilirkkat teṉṟal ulāvac
	cantaṉam maṇakkum taṇṇiya aruvikūr
10	potiyat tamarntu puviyelām pōṟṟa
	matinalam vaḷarkkum māṇpami muttamiḻ
	nitivaḷam nalkum nikarilā mātavaṉ
	akattiyaṟ karuḷiya takavami ilakkaṇa
	mikapperuṅ kaliyic cakattavar uṇara
15	palkāp piyanteḷi tolkāp piyaṉmutal
	olkāp pulami oṇṭamiḻ nūlōr
	iyampiya ilakkaṇap paṉuval yāviyum
	uḷanterin turicey iḷampū raṇarmutal
	cēṉā variyar, teyvac ciliyār,
20	āṉāp pulamip pērā ciriyar
	uccimēṟ pulavarkoḷ nacciṉārk kiṉiyar
	vicci mikkanak kīra ṉāroṭu
	civañāṉa muṉivar, cuvāmi nātaṉār
	yāppa ruṅkala virutti mutalāya
25	urivaḻi yāviyum puriyaṟak kaṟṟaṟintu,
	ariyavi yāviyum cintiyiṟ ṟeḷintu
	kaṟṟati maṟavāp peṟṟiyoṭu tikaḻum
	aruntiṟaṟ pulavaṉ, perumpē rācāṉ,
	virunteṉa vaṭamoḻi payiṉṟaṟi tiṟalōṉ,
30	āṅkilap pulamicīr tāṅkiṭum ācāṉ,
	caṅka ilakkiyac cālpoṭu iraṭṭik
	kāppiya nuṇukkamum cintā maṇiyiṉit
	tērntaṟin tuvanturi virikkum cemmal,
	tēṅkamaḻ amiḻteṉa māṇavar cevikoḷap
35	pāṭam payiṟṟum paṇpami ācāṉ
	tirumuṟi, tivviyap pirapanta aruḷvaḷam
	niṟimoḻi yāvum neñcam iṉikka
	uruccey tuvakkum oḷirtamiḻp pāvalaṉ,
	purāṇa itikācap pulamiyum nuṭpam
40	virāviya kampaṉ kavitiyum piṟapiṟa
	ciṟṟilak kiyakkaṭal tiḷittanaṟ kalvimāṉ,
	āḷuṭip piḷḷiyum aracum nampiyum
	tāḷuṟac cūḻntu taliyuṟap paṇintu
	teyvat tamiḻāṟ puṉintatē vāram
45	meyyaṇin tuvakkum aiyā ṟaṉtikaḻ
	kāvirit tāyiṉ karimici yoḷicey
	tiruviyā ṟataṉiṟ centamiḻt tāyiṉ
	uḷḷam uvappa utitta taṉayaṉ,
	antaṇar kulattil vantanaṟ cāṉṟōṉ
50	kuṇattāl uyarnta kōpā liyaṉ,
	aṉpum, aṭakkamum, naṇpami ceyalum,
	iṉcolum, eḷimiyum, iyalpō ṭamintoḷir
	pōtakā ciriyaṉ, putiyaṉa puṉiyum
	āyvaṟi vāḷaṉ, arumpeṟaṟ kaṭṭuri
55	tarumeḻut tāḷaṉ, mūvartē vārac
	colvaḷam icivaḷam malkiṭum ñāṉam,
	malarntiṭum kaṟpaṉi, vaṇṇaṉi uḷḷam
	kalantiṭum paktik kavinayam yāviyum
	ulakuṇarn tuyya urivirit tiyampiya
60	palakali yuṇarnta paṇṭitaṉ ilakkaṇak
	kaṭalpaṭin telli nilikaṇ ṭeḻunta
	ācāṉ, taṉpē ruḻippiṉāl ilakkaṇak
	kaliccol yāviyum kaviṉuṟat tokuttup
	poruḷnili viḷaṅka akara niralpaṭa
65	ilakkaṇap pēraka rātiyi varintu
	pērā ciriyar,āy vāḷar, māṇavar
	yāvarum payaṉpeṟak kāviyam pōlat
	tamiḻttāyk kaṇiyāt takavuṟa vaḻaṅkum
	paṇṭita ti.vē. kōpā liyar
70	vaṇṭamiḻ pōla vaḷamelām mēvi
	maṇṭalam pukaḻap pallāṇṭu pallāṇṭu
	vāḻkeṉap pōṟṟi vāḻttutum uvanteṉ.

tañcāvūr		
22.08.2005					 pāvalarēṟu ca. pālacuntaraṉār


“kaṉi tantāl kaṉi
uṇṇavum vallirō”
mutuperum pulavar ilakkaṇak kaṭal uyartiruvāḷar 
ti.vē. kōpālaṉār, tamiḻvaḷak koṭiyāka, arumpatippup peruntaki kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār vaḻaṅkum, ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’ maṭalaṅkaḷ patiṉēḻaṉiyum oruṅkē kaṇṭatum,
“kārē! nērē tāṉ,
vāriyuṇṭu; vārimoṇṭu vāriyuṇṭu, vāṉiruṇṭu
pēri koṇṭu nītiraṇṭu pey”
eṉṉum, vāṉmaḻip pāṭṭiṉ ‘tēṉpiḻi’veṉa eṉakku avi iṉpa mūṭṭiṉa.
kōpālaṉār mūḷik kūrppum,
iḷavaḻakaṉār patippu īrppum,
oruṅkē vayappaṭuttiya iṉpattil,
‘aṉṉi vāḻka’, ‘ayarāt toṇṭarkaḷum vāḻka’
eṉa eṉṉuḷ vāḻttiṉēṉ.
kōpālaṉār niṉiviṉ ēntal; ilakkaṇamā ilakkiyamā, nūlōṭu uriyum neñcak kaḷaṉil varappaṭutti vippāka vittuk kākkum karuvūla vāḻvar.
kaṟṟatu oṉṟiyum kiviṭāk ‘karumi’yeṉak kavarntu koṇṭa tamiḻvaḷatti, eṉṟum eṅkum evarkkum ‘tarumi’ eṉa vāri vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷiyar. avartam ivvakarātik koṭi, tamiḻulaku kālattāl peṟṟa kaviṉ paricilām!
eṉṉi yaṟiyātē eṉakkoru perumitam; ēkkaḻuttam; “ittaku pāriya ilakkaṇat tokutikaḷi immoḻi oṉṟi yaṉṟi, emmoḻitāṉ peṟakkūṭum?” eṉṉum eṇṇattiṉ viḷivē aḵtām!
ammamma!
eḻuttu	-	iraṇṭu maṭalaṅkaḷ
col	-	nāṉku maṭalaṅkaḷ
iv āṟu maṭalaṅkaḷit tāṉē maṟṟi maṟṟi moḻikaḷ peṟakkūṭum! poruḷilakkaṇam eṉpatoṉṟu koḷḷā moḻikaḷ, eppaṭit tamiḻip pōl poruḷ ilakkaṇa maṭalaṅkaḷip peṟa vāykkum?
poruḷilakkaṇa maṭalaṅkaḷ, eḻuttu, col maṭalaṅ kaḷip pōṉṟa eṇṇikkiyiṉavō?
akapporuḷ		-	nāṉku maṭalaṅkaḷ.
puṟapporuḷ		-	oru maṭalam
yāppu			-	iraṇṭu maṭalaṅkaḷ
aṇi			-	iraṇṭu maṭalaṅkaḷ
meyppāṭu mutalaṉa	-	oru maṭalam
pāṭṭiyal mutalaṉa	-	oru maṭalam
ākap patiṉoru maṭalaṅkaḷ. mottamākak kūṭutal 17 maṭalaṅkaḷ.
moḻi ārvalarkkup ‘perumitam’ uṇṭākumā? uṇṭākātā?
ipperumitattūṭēyē ōr ‘ēkkam’:
ariya āyvuk kuṟippukaḷ vaḻaṅkit teḷivuṟutta valla āciriyar, avaṟṟi aritāka mēṟkoṇṭati aṉṟip peritāka allatu muṟṟāka mēṟkoṇṭilarē eṉpatē avvēkkam.
e-ṭu:
‘akara mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy’ eṉpaṉa iḷampūraṇarukku aṉmoḻit toki; nacciṉārkkiṉiyarukku eḻuvāyt toṭarkaḷ - eṉṟu kāṭṭum tokiyāciriyar,
“akaratti mutalākavum ṉakaratti iṟutiyākavum uṭiya eṉṟu poruḷ ceyyiṉ akaramutal - ṉakara iṟuvāy eṉpaṉa iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit toki” eṉṟu tam teḷivi iyikkiṟār (eḻuttu 1:22)
naṉṉūlār aḷapeṭiyic cārpeḻuttāka eṇṇiṉār. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār aḷapeṭiyi uyireḻuttuḷ aṭakkik koṇṭār eṉpatic civañāṉa muṉivar cūttira virutti koṇṭu teḷivikkiṟār tokiyāciriyar. (eḻuttu 1:44)
“makarak kuṟukkam mēlāl peṟum puḷḷiyoṭu uḷḷum puḷḷi peṟum eṉṟa uriyāciriyar karuttu mikat teḷḷitu. ‘uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvākummē’ (to.e. 14) eṉṟa nūṟpāviṟku uṇmiyuri kāṇa utavukiṟatu” eṉṟu teḷivikkiṟār toki yāciriyar. (eḻuttu 2: 263)
‘vēṇṭā kūṟi vēṇṭiyatu muṭittal’, eṉṉum talippil, “tēviyaṟṟatu pōṉṟa oru ceytiyik kūṟi ataṉāl, nūṟpākkaḷil kūṟappaṭāta maṟṟōr iṉṟiyamiyāta ceytiyip peṟappaṭa vittal” eṉṟu viḷakkum tokiyāciriyar, nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulam kaṟpavar teḷivukku nalvaḻi kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. (eḻuttu 2:265)
āṉāl, ittakiyavi peritum iṭam peṟāmal ‘toki yaḷavoṭu’ niṉṟu viṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatē avvēkkam. toki yāvatu, tokuppu.
‘kuṟṟiyalukaram oṟṟu īṟē’ eṉpatōr āyvu eṉṟāl,
‘kuṟṟiyalukaram uyir īṟē’ eṉpatōr āyvu ātal kāṭṭappaṭavēṇṭum allavō! etu cevvitu eṉpatit teḷivittal iṉṟēṉum, teḷivikkak karuvitantatu ākum aṉṟō! ivvāṟu, piṉṉāyvāḷar eṇṇaṟṟōr āyvukaḷ koḷḷappaṭāmi maṭṭumaṉṟu; taḷḷappaṭutal mikat teḷi vākiṉṟatu.
tokiyāciriyarāl cuṭṭappaṭum aracañcaṇmukaṉār (pāṭṭiyal 15) āyvu ettakiyatu eṉiṉ, ‘naṭunili piṟaḻāmal ārāyntu eḻutuvatil vallunar’ eṉat tokiyāciriyarālēyē pārāṭṭappaṭum takiyatām. accaṇmukaṉār, ‘piramāṇam ākāta nūlkaḷ’ eṉpatiyum pativu ceykiṟār tokiyāciriyar. atu,
“ciṉṉūl(nēminātam), naṉṉūl, vīracōḻiyam, ilakkaṇak kottu, pirayōka vivēkam, cūttiravirutti, ilakkaṇa viḷakkac cūṟāvaḷi, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam mutalākap pala, tolkāppiyattiṉ vaḻippaṭac ceyyappaṭiṉum āciriyaṉatu karuttuṇarāmal marapu nili tiriyac ceyyappaṭṭamiyāṉ piramāṇamākāta nūlkaḷ ām. (piramāṇam nūlneṟikkuc cāṉṟāka - eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat tikaḻum vāymiyākiya takuti; niyāya aḷavikaḷāl uṟutippāṭu) ivvāṟu kuṟippar aracañcaṇmukaṉār (pā.vi. pak. 104-105)” eṉkiṟār (pāṭ. 142) eṉpatu.
tokiyāciriyar, ic cāṉṟip poṉṉē pōlap pōṟṟiyiruppiṉ, ataṉ peruñciṟappu eppaṭi iruntirukkum? taṉ peyarukku ēṟpat ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’ eṉpataṟkut tāṉē taṉṉikarilāt talimi koṇṭirukkum!
ippiramāṇamākā nūlkaḷit taḷḷiyiruppiṉ ippāriya nili irukkumō eṉiṉ, avveṇṇam piḻipaṭṭa eṇṇa mām!
‘toṭarnilic ceyyuḷvakip peyar’ eṉṉum talippil kūṟappaṭum aḷavukōl tāṉā iṉṟum uḷḷatu? caturakarātiyum pirapanta marapiyalum kūṟuvaṉa 96 maṭṭumē. maṟṟi nūlkaḷāl aṟivaṉa ataṟkum uṭpaṭṭaṉavē. āṉāl, ciṟṟilakkiya virivākkap parappu ettaṉi? 381 vakiyeṉpati em, ‘ilakkiya vakimi akarāti’yil kāṇalām. avaṟṟiṉ mēlum itukāl virin tuḷḷaṉa. poruḷatikāra āyvō, veḷḷap perukkāki uḷḷatu. kālṭuvelār, ñāṉap pirakāca aṭikaḷār, pāvāṇar aṉṉavarkaḷ āyvu tamiḻilakkaṇa āyvukaḷ alla eṉat taḷḷap paṭṭuviṭāvē! avaṟṟi nōkkiṉāl, vēṇṭāc cērppukaḷi vilakki, vēṇṭum cērppukaḷi iṇittuk koṇṭāl iṉṉum patiṉ maṭalaṅkaḷ mikum eṉpati, niṉiviṉ ēntal - cōrvaṟiyāc cuṭar - kōpālaṉār koḷvārē eṉiṉ, ittamiḻ ilakkaṇappērakarāti ottatōr akarāti iṉṟām eṉa malimēl ēṟi muḻakkamiṭalām allavō!
ittokiyāciriyap perumakaṉāri allār oruvar, ipperuṅ kaṭappāṭṭi mēṟkoṇṭu ittaku paṇi ceytal aritu! avartam muḻutuṟu oppaṭippiṉ pēṟu atu. aṉṟiyum, tammip pōlavē tam uṭaṉpiṟappukaḷiyum aḻuntip payilavum ārvak kaṭaṉāṟṟavum payiṟṟi irukkum payiṟṟutaṟpēṟu; tamiḻ vāḻvākiya avarit tāṅkutalē tam piṟavip pēṟu eṉakkoṇṭu nayattaku tuṇiyāyum kuṭumpamākiyum niṟpār keḻutakip pēṟu; iṉṉavellām, it tamiḻp peruṅkoṭikku ūṟṟuk kaṇkaḷ aṉṉavām.
iṅkuc cuṭṭappaṭṭavi, tamiḻk kātalāl, tamiḻar paṇ pāṭṭuk kātalāl cuṭṭappaṭṭavi eṉpatik kaṟpār uṇariṉ, ittokutikaḷit tattam kuṭimi vippākak koḷḷak kaṭamippaṭṭavarām. āyvukku ippaṭiyoru karuvi eḷitil vāykkumā? āyvukku elli uṇṭā? ‘aṟitō ṟaṟiyāmi’ kāṇum āyvukku, “maṉitarkāḷ iṅkēvam; kaṉitantāl kaṉi uṇṇavum vallirō” eṉṟu apparaṭikaḷ tamiḻkkōyil vāyil muṉ niṉṟu aḻittu vaḻikāṭṭuvatu pōla, ayarāt toṇ ṭarkaḷ ti.vē. kōpālaṉārum, kō. iḷavaḻakaṉārum it tokiyik kikōttu niṉṟu kaṉivoṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar! nām pēṟeṉap peṟṟup payaṉ koḷvōmāka!

tamiḻttoṇṭaṉ,
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


‘kuṟuntaṭṭāka’ viḷaṅkupavar
paṇṭitavittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar
putuvikku varukiṉṟavarkaḷ iṅkē iraṇṭu kaṭalkaḷip pārkkalām. oṉṟu uvarkkaṭal; maṟṟoṉṟu ti.vē. kōpāliyar eṉṉum nūṟkaṭal. ‘takaṭupaṭu pacumpoṟ cikaraṅkaḷiṉ mukaṭu toṭuttup poṉkoḻittu, maṇivaraṉṟi, māṇikkattoṭu vayiram unti vantu, cala calaveṉa iḻitarum aṇikiḷar aruvi’ pōṉṟa ivaratu poḻiviṉi oru muṟi kēṭkum evarum viyantu, ivar oru nūṟkaṭaltām’ eṉpati eḷitiṉil ēṟṟukkoḷvar.
‘aḷakkalākā aḷavum poruḷum tuḷakkalākā niliyum tōṟṟamum’ koṇṭa maliyaṉiya māṇpiṉ aṟiviṉarākiya ti.vē. kōpāliyar kaṟṟōrtamakku varampākiya takimiyar. taṇṭamiḻiṉ mēlāntaramāṉa ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum, avaṟṟukkup paṇṭiyōr uritta taṇṭamiḻ urikaḷiyum iḷamiyilēyē pativu ceytukoṇṭa kuṟuntaṭṭāka viḷaṅku pavar ip peruntaki. ekkāraṇattālāvatu innūlkaḷil oṉṟi iḻakka nēriṉ kavalippaṭa vēṇṭiyatilli; ivartam uḷḷap pativiliruntu ataṉi mīṇṭum uruvākkik koḷḷalām.
‘centamiḻt teṉputuvi eṉṉum tirunakarkkup’ pukaḻ cērtta pāratiyār, pāvēntar mutaliya pukaḻmaṇikaḷiṉ variciyil iṉṟu kōpāliyar viṇṇuyar tōṟṟattuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṟār. iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, pērāciriyar, teyvacciliyār, kallāṭar, parimēlaḻakar, civañāṉa muṉivar, kārirattiṉak kavirāyar mutaliya pulavar marapiṉōr pukaḻi yellām tam pukaḻ ākkikkoṇṭa ippulavar peruntakiyip putuvip pulavarulakam pōṟṟi ‘nūṟkaṭal’ eṉṟa ciṟappup paṭṭamaḷittup poṉpōṟ potintu koṇṭatu.
irupattāṟāṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ivar putuvip pirañcuk kali niṟuvaṉattil (viuṭiṭana kucayanḷīyaḷina) tamiḻāyvup piriviṉ talimip poṟuppiṉi mēṟkoṇṭu ariya nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟi āyvuc cempatippukaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟār.
pāratiyāriṉ taliciṟanta paṭippukaḷukku vāyppāka irunta putuvimaṇ, kōpāliyariṉ pukaḻi eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavac ceyyum uyar patippukaḷ pala uruvāvataṟkum vāytta iṭamāka ilaṅkukiṟatu.
kaṭanta paṉṉīrāṇṭukaḷākap putuviyiliruntu veḷivantu koṇṭirukkum ‘teḷitamiḻ’ eṉṉum tamiḻ vaḷarccit tiṅkaḷi taḻil, ivartam paṭippiṉi ēnti vārāta itaḻē illi. atil ‘ili maṟi kaṉikaḷ’ eṉṉum talippil, tamiḻilakkaṇa ilakkiya nūlkaḷilum urikaḷilum ili maṟi kaṉikaḷāka maṟin tirukkum ariya ceytikaḷit tiraṭṭik kaṭṭurikaḷākat tantu varukiṟār.
alāṉ taṉiyēlu (haṭayaṉē ṉuyanniṭaṭira) eṉṉum mēṉāṭṭaṟiñariṉ maṇimēkali āṅkila moḻipeyarppup paṇikkut tuṇi ceytatum, cēṉāvariyattiṉ pirañcu moḻiyākkattiṟkut tuṇi niṉṟatum ivartam āṅkila aṟivukkuc cāṉṟu pakarum.
‘tolkāppiyap paḻiya urikaḷiṉ cempatippu’, ‘kal veṭṭukkaḷil nāyaṉmārkaḷ paṟṟiya aruñ ceytikaḷ’ ākiyaṉa iṉi veḷivara irukkum ivartam nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavi.
ivartam paṇikaḷil mikamikap payaṉ viḷikkum ariya periya paṇi inta ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’. tamiḻilakkaṇam kaṟpōrukkum, ilakkaṇa āyvāḷarkaḷukkum ki viḷakkākap payaṉpaṭakkūṭiya iv vakaravarici irupattintu āṇṭuk kāla pēruḻippāl eḻuti muṭikkappeṟṟatu. eppōtu veḷivarumō eṉṟu tamiḻaṟiñar ulakam etirpārttiruntatu.
maṟṟavar ariyateṉṟu karutum nalla patippup paṇikaḷi eḷiyateṉṟu ēṟṟu, maṭitaṟṟut tāmē muṉvantu ceyyum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar, ‘tamiḻmoḻik kāvalar’ 
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ itaṉi aḻakuṟap patippittu vaḻaṅkukiṟār. ivvariya ceyalāl, iṉpattamiḻ irukkumaḷavum iḷavaḻakaṉ pukaḻum irukkum eṉpatu uṟuti.
	aṉpaṉ,
	irā. tirumurukaṉ.


‘īrattamiḻil āḻaṅkāl paṭṭavar’
paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar
ulakap potumaṟiyi aruḷiya tiruvaḷḷuvap perun takiyār periyār yār eṉpataṟku ōr ilakkaṇam vakukkiṉṟār. maṉitamēmpāṭṭukkuriyatāṉa, ceyvataṟku ariyatāṉa ceyali yār purikiṉṟārkaḷō avarkaḷē periyavar eṉkiṉṟār.
kālaṅkaḷtōṟum palvēṟu tuṟikaḷil maṉitamēmpāṭṭuk kākap palar ceyaṟkariya ceyalkaḷic ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. anta variciyil tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti eṉṉum innūli ti.vē.kōpāliyarum, inta nūli veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar iḷavaḻakaṉārum aṭaṅkuvar.
tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ orucamayam illāmaṟ pōy viṭṭālum kōpāliyar oruvar iruntālē pōtum, avaṟṟi mīṭṭuk koṇṭuvantuviṭalām. anta aḷaviṟkut tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷit tam niṉivil vittiruppavar.
oru tēṉkūṭṭil pala malarkaḷiṉ tēṉkaḷ kalantiruppatu pōl intap pērakarātiyil pala tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāciriyarkaḷiṉ variyaṟikaḷum pala uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ uri vaḷaṅkaḷum kalantuḷḷaṉa.
aṟiñar tilakam kōpāliyar eppaṭi eḷimiyāṉava rākavum, āḻamāṉa pulami uṭiyavarākavum, paḻaka iṉiyavarākavum irukkiṟārō, appaṭiyē ‘naviltoṟum nūlnayam pōlum payiltoṟum paṇpuṭiyāḷar toṭarpu’ eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷiṉ karuttukkēṟpa innūlum nammiṭam paḻakukiṉṟatu.
inta nūl pērakarātiyāka uḷḷatāl ilakkaṇam kaṇṭu añcum māṇavarkaḷum tamiḻ ārvalarkaḷum potumakkaḷum akarātip poruḷi aṟivatu pōl eḷitākat tamiḻ ilakkaṇatti aṟintu koḷḷa muṭiyumāṟu uḷḷatu.
innūl tolkāppiyar kālattiliruntu inta nāḷvari uḷḷa tamiḻ ulakiṟkuk kiṭitta putumiyāṉa mutaṉmiyāṉa muḻumiyāṉa nūlākum.
palar muyaṉṟu ceyya vēṇṭiya paṇiyi ti.vē.kōpā liyarē ceytu muṭittuḷḷār. oru palkalikaḻakamō oru periya āyvu niṟuvaṉamō ceyya vēṇṭiya paṇiyit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam ārvattāl eḷitākac ceytuḷḷatu. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tavappayaṉē ippaṭiyāyt tamiḻ maṇṇil mukiḻttuḷḷatu.
tamiḻakattil maṭṭumallāmal tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum aṉittu iṭaṅkaḷilum tirukkuṟaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka nūlakaṅkaḷil iṭampeṟa vēṇṭiya tamiḻnūl innūl eṉpatil aiyam illi.
inta nūli veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka iḷavaḻaka ṉārkku namatu veṟṟi vāḻttukkaḷ. inta nūli aḷittaruḷiya aṟiñartilakam nammuṭiya ilakkaṇa māmaṇi kōpāliyarukkut taliyallāl kimmāṟilli  eṉṉum paṭiyāṉa tamiḻ vaṇakkaṅkaḷ. vāḻka tamiḻ. vaḷarka tamiḻppaṇpāṭu. velka maṉitanēyam.

aṭiyēṉ.
muṉivar ‘viṇavam’ pārttacārati


‘māntak kaṇiṉi’
paṇṭitavittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar
nām caṅkap pulavarkaḷip pārttilōm! iṭik kālap pulavarkaḷiyum uri vallunarkaḷiyum pārttilōm! āṉāl avarkaḷi yellām nam kālattil pārttiṭa virumpuvōmāyiṉ avarkaḷiṉ uruvāka vāḻntukoṇṭirukkum nūṟkaṭal ti. vē. kōpāliyar avarkaḷik kāṇalē cālum.
cāti, mata, vayatu vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tammi aṇukum yārē yāyiṉum ayarvuṟātu māṟṟam koḷḷātu avartam aiyaṅkaṭkut teḷivēṟpaṭuttalum viṉākkaṭku viṭiyaḷittalumāṉa cīriya tamiḻppaṇiyit toṭarntu āṟṟivarukiṟār.
enta nūlil, entap pakkattil, entap pākiyil, enta variyil uḷḷatu eṉat teḷinta uṇarvuṭaṉ entac colliyum karuttiyum cuṭṭik kāṭṭum viyakkattakka māntak kaṇiṉiyāka viḷaṅkukiṟār.
caṅkap pulavarum iṭik kālap pulavarum uri vallunarum kiyāṇṭa moḻi mutaleḻuttu, moḻiyiṟuti eḻuttu, puṇarcci neṟikaḷ, piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷiyum eḻuttukkaḷiyum nīkkal ākiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇa marapukaḷip poṉṉē pōl pōṟṟip payaṉpaṭutti varum pāṅku eṇṇi eṇṇi makiḻattakkatākum.
apperumakaṉār vāḻuṅkālattil vāḻtalum, avariṭattu uriyāṭi makiḻtalum, ēṟpaṭum aiyaṅkaṭku avariṉ collariya viḷakkaṅkaḷik kēṭṭut teḷitalumākiya ariya pēṟṟiṉi nāṉ eṉatu vāḻnāḷil peṟṟuḷḷēṉ.
avariṉ paranta ilakkiya nūlaṟivum teḷinta ilakkaṇa aṟivum tamiḻarkkum ulakukkum mēlum payaṉpaṭutal vēṇṭum.
avar mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa eḷiya vāḻvu, cama nōkku, iṉiya pēccu, ellārkkum payaṉpaṭal vēṇṭum eṉṉum perumpaṇpu ākiyavi kaṟṟāri īrkkum taṉmiyaṉa.
nūṟkaṭalār mēlum pallāṇṭukaḷ vāḻntu tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarccikkup peruntoṇṭāṟṟiṭal vēṇṭum eṉa eṉatu uḷḷam niṟinta viḻiviṉit terivittukkoḷkiṟēṉ.
aṉpaṉ
iṟiviḻiyaṉ


patippuri
tamiḻar vāḻviyal ilakkaṇatti variyaṟuttuk kūṟum olkāppukaḻ tolkāppiyattiṟkup paḻantamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷ paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu eḻutiya aṉittu urikaḷiyum tokuttu tolkāppiya nūl patippil ituvari evarum kaṇṭirāta vakiyil orēvīccil em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭati tamiḻulakam naṉku aṟiyum.
tolkāppiya nūlpatippup paṇikku allum pakalum tuṇi yiruntu uḻittavar paṇṭitavittuvāṉ ti.vē.kōpāliyar āvār. innūl patippukaḷukku ivarē patippāciriyarāka iruntu em tamiḻp paṇikkup perumi cērttavar. kūraṟivum, perum pulamiyum, niṉivāṟṟalum mikka ipperuntaki irupattintu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka tam pēruḻippāl tokutta tamiḻ ilak kaṇattiṟkāṉa col mūlaṅkaḷi ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’ eṉum perumpaṭippāka 17 tokutikaḷi uḷḷaṭakki ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇa’k koṭiyāka tamiḻ ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūl patippu varalāṟṟil tamiḻ ilakkaṇat tiṟkeṉa orē nērattil eḻuttu, col, poruḷ (akam, puṟam, aṇi, yāppu, pāṭṭiyal, pāyiram, marapiyal, meyppāṭu, nāṭakam, aḷavi niyāyam) eṉum variciyil pērakarāti veḷivaruvatu ituvē mutal muṟiyākum.
akarāti eṉpatu oru collukku viḷakkam taruvatu. pērakarāti eṉpatu oru collukku viḷakkamum, mēṟkōḷum cuṭṭuvatu. kaḷañciyam eṉpatu oru collukkup palporuḷ viḷakkam kāṭṭuvatu.
uṅkaḷ kikaḷil tavaḻum it tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarātit tokutikaḷ tamiḻukkuk karuvūlamāy amivatākum. tamiḻilakkaṇap perum parappi virivu ceyyum ip peṭṭakatti veḷiyiṭuvatil perumip paṭukiṟōm. tamiḻ āyvi mēṟkoḷvārkku virac curaṅkamākavum, tamiḻ ārvalarkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷi viḷak kākavum innūl tokutikaḷ varukiṟatu.
toṉmiyum, muṉmiyum, tāymiyum, tūymiyum, meṉmiyum, mēṉmiyum, paḻamiyum, putumiyum, iḷamiyum, mutumiyum mikkamoḻi nam tamiḻmoḻi. tiraviṭamoḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi nam tamiḻmoḻi. intiyap perumoḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻi nam tamiḻmoḻi. ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi nam tamiḻmoḻi. intiyap pēraracāl cemmoḻi eṉa aṟivikkap paṭṭuḷḷa in nērattil, ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’yi veḷiyiṭuvatil peru makiḻcci aṭikiṟōm.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇac curaṅkattit tantuḷḷa paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyaruṭaṉ uṭaṉiruntu  uḻittavar avar iḷaval ti.vē. kaṅkātaraṉ āvār. ip poṟkuviyal polivōṭu veḷi varuvataṟku tam muḻup poḻutiyum celaviṭṭavar ivar. iraveṉṟum, pakaleṉṟum pārātu uḻitta ip perumakka ḷukkum, pērakarātiyiṉ arumi perumikaḷi matippīṭu aḷittu perumi cērtta putuccēri pireñcu intiya āyvu niṟuvaṉat tukkum, tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkkum em naṉṟi.

accu
	etirmam (cūnapayaviena)	:	pirāsas intiyā, ceṉṉi
	accu maṟṟum kaṭṭamippu	:	veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭars, ceṉṉi

ivarkaḷukku em naṉṟi . 


aṇi 1





a
akaṭaṉa kaṭam -
itu māṇikkavācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (120) varuvatōr aṇi.
aṟiñar uritta ceyyuḷil kuṟṟaṅkaḷ kāṭṭip piṉ avi illi eṉa urippatu itu.
e-ṭu :	‘kamparāmāyaṇa mutaṟpāṭal caṅkōttara virutti’ pōlvaṉa .
akamalarcciyaṇi -
oru poruḷiṉuṭiya kuṇattālum kuṟṟattālum maṟṟoru poruḷukkum avi ēṟpaṭṭaṉavākac collutal aka malarcciyaṇiyām. iḵtu ullāsālaṅkāram eṉa vaṭa nūluḷ kūṟappaṭum. ivvaṇi nāṉku vakiyākap pirittu uṇarappaṭum. aviyāvaṉa:
1. kuṇattiṉāl kuṇattic collum akamalarcciyaṇi
2. kuṇattiṉāl kuṟṟattic collum akamalarcciyaṇi
3. kuṟṟattiṉāl kuṇattic collum akamalarcciyaṇi
4. kuṟṟattiṉāl kuṟṟattic collum akamalarcciyaṇi eṉpaṉa	(ca. 9, kuva. 69, mu.vī. poruḷ aṇi 53)
	1. kuṇattiṉāl kuṇattic collum akamalarcciyaṇi
oru poruḷiṉuṭiya kuṇattāl maṟṟoru poruḷukkum akkuṇam ēṟpaṭuvatākac collum akamalarcci aṇi vaki itu.
e-ṭu :	
ikkaṟ piṉaḷmūḻkit tūymi ceyumōeṉṟu
akkaṅki koḷḷum avā’
palarum kaṅkiyil mūḻkit tampāvattip pōkkik koḷvatāl, kaṅki palaruṭiya pāvaṅkaḷiyum cumantu taṉ tūymi kuṟintuviṭṭatu pōṉṟa eṇṇam koḷva tāyiṟṟu. tūymiyuṭiya ikkaṟpuṭiya peṇ taṉṉiṭam vantu mūḻkutalāl tāṉ cumantirukkum pāvaṅkaḷellām toliyat tāṉum tūymi peṟalām eṉṟu kaṅki āṟu ācippaṭukiṟatu eṉṉum toṭaril, kaṟpiṉaḷatu tūymip paṇpu kaṅkikkum ēṟpaṭum eṉṟu karututal ivvaṇi vaki yākum.
irāmaṉ kaṅkiyāṟṟil cītiyuṭaṉ muḻkiya ceytiyik kampar,
“kaṉṉi nīkka(a)ruṅ kaṅkiyum kitoḻā,
paṉṉi nīkka (a) rum pātakam, pāruḷōr
eṉṉiṉ nīkkuvar; yāṉumiṉṟu eṟṟanta
uṉṉiṉ nīkkiṉēṉ, uyntaṉeṉ yāṉ”   eṉṟāḷ. 
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa pāṭaluḷḷum ivvaṇiyik kāṇalām.	(kamparā. 1942.)
	2. kuṇattiṉāl kuṟṟattic collum akamalarcciyaṇi
oru poruḷiṭattuḷḷa kuṇattik koṇṭu maṟṟoru poruḷukku oru kuṟṟatti ēṟṟikkūṟum akamalarcci aṇivaki itu.
e-ṭu :	“naṉmakkaḷi vaṟumiyil āḻttit tīyōric celvarāka ākkum poṟṟāmariyil vīṟṟirukkum tirumakaḷ aḻintu cāmpuka“
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭum
‘nāṟāt takaṭēpōl naṉmalarmēl poṟpāvāy!
nīṟāy nilattu viḷiyarō! - vēṟāya
puṉmakkaḷ pakkam pukuvāy nī; poṉpōlum
naṉmakkaḷ pakkam tuṟantu’
eṉṟa pāṭalil, naṉmakkaḷ vaṟumiyilum cemmiyuṭiya rāy vāḻum kuṇattik kaṇṭu, avarkaḷi aṭintu avarkaḷuṭiya vaṟumiyip pōkkāta tirumakaḷuṭiya kuṟṟam kuṟippiṭap paṭṭiruppatu ivvaṇiyām.
	3. kuṟṟattiṉāl kuṇattic collum akamalarcciyaṇi -
oru poruḷiṉuṭiya kuṟṟattik kuṟippiṭṭu apporuḷi ṉuṭiya kuṟṟattiṉāl maṟṟoru poruḷukkuk kuṇam tōṉṟuvatākac collum akamalarcci aṇi vaki itu.
e-ṭu :	veṟṟipeṟum maṉṉaṉ pakimaṉṉarik kollā mal viṭuttatē avarkaḷukkuc ciṟanta pēṟākum eṉṟa karuttaminta
‘kōaṭuvēl koṟṟava!nī kollāmi viṭṭatuvē
cēvakarkkup pērū tiyam’
eṉṉum pāṭalil, pakivarkaḷ puṟamutukiṭṭu ōṭiṉar eṉṟa kuṟṟattiṉkaṇ, puṟamutukiṭṭu ōṭupavarit turatticceṉṟu kollāmiyākiya aracaṉuṭiya naṟpaṇpu pulaṉākiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟutal ivvaṇivakiyākum.
	4. kuṟṟattiṉāl kuṟṟattic collum akamalarcciyaṇi
oru poruḷiṉuṭiya kuṟṟattāl maṟṟoru poruḷukkum akkuṟṟam uṇṭāvatākac collum akamalarcciyaṇi vaki itu.
e-ṭu :	“aracaṉuṭiya pakivarkaḷiṉ maṉivimār tam ūriliruntu uyirtappik kāṭṭuvaḻiyē ōṭum pōtu tam melliya pātaṅkaḷ naṭattal āṟṟātu varuntit tam nakilkaḷukku vakutta aḻuttattit tam pātaṅ kaḷukku vakukkāta piramaṉip paḻittuk kaṇṇīr viṭuvar”eṉṟa karuttu aminta
‘ciṉakkatirvēl vēntē!niṉ tevvar maṭavār
vaṉattilaṭin tōṭuṅkāl, māḻkit - taṉattiluṟa
vittaperun tiṭpamaṭi viyāta nāṉmukaṉi
mittaviḻi nīrukavi vār’
eṉṉum pāṭalil, pātaṅkaḷ meṉmiyavāka aminta kuṟṟattāl, avaṟṟip paṭitta nāṉmukaṉukkum aṟivu melinta kuṟṟam ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟutal ivvaṇi vakiyākum.
akāraṇamikimoḻi -
mikimoḻi - aticaya aṇi. kāraṇam kāṭṭātu payaṉ paṭuttappaṭum aticaya aṇi. itu ‘akāraṇa mikimoḻi aṇi’ eṉṟu oru cārārāl koḷḷappaṭum.	(vī. cō. 177)
e-ṭu :	‘turumam cantiraṟ ṟōytiruk kāḷatti’  (cīkā.pu.kaṭavuḷ)
kāḷatti maliyil cōliyiluḷḷa maraṅkaḷ cantiraṉit tōyvaṉa pōla uyarntuḷḷaṉa eṉṟa karuttaminta ivvaṭiyil, oru kāraṇamumiṉṟi maraṅkaḷatu uyarcci aticayaaṇipaṟṟi mikuttuk kūṟappaṭṭavāṟu.
accaccuvi -
itu cuviyaṇiyiṉ eṇvakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; uḷḷattil accam tōṉṟuvatāl viḷiyum meyppāṭukaḷik kūṟuvatu.
e-ṭu :	‘kinerittu veytuyirppak kāltaḷarntu mey paṉippa miyarikkaṇ nīrtatumpa vāypularntāḷ - tiyal ciṉavēl viṭaliyāl kiyiḻanta ceṅkaṇ puṉavēḻam mēlvanta pōtu’
ciṉamikka vēṟpaṭiyi ēntiya vīraṉāl kiveṭṭuṇṭa kāṭṭuyāṉi taṉmuṉṉē tuṭumeṉa vantuṟṟatāka, atu pōtu oru mātu taṉkikaḷi nerittukkoṇṭu, peru mūccuṇṭāka, kālnaṭi taḷarappeṟṟu, uṭal naṭuṅkāniṟpa, taṉṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷil nīr tuḷumpa vāyularntu pōṉāḷ - eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ippāṭaṟkaṇ, yāṉiyik kaṇṭa tāl uḷḷattil uṇṭāṉa accam paṟṟiya meyppāṭukaḷ collappaṭṭavāṟu kāṇka.	(taṇṭi. 70-2)
accaccuvi uvamam -
uvamam kūṟumpōtu eṇvakic cuvikaḷum uṭaṉiyiya, uvamam kūṟutal ciṟappuṭittu. eṇvakic cuvikaḷil accaccuvi oṉṟām. accuvi tōṉṟa varum uvamam itu.
e-ṭu :	oruvaric cāṉṟōr eṉa matittu uṟavu koṇ ṭāṭumpōtu avariṭam cāṉṟāṇmi illāta nili, oru ceppiṉuḷ cantaṉam irukkum eṉṟu karutic ceppiṉit tiṟantaaḷavil ataṉuḷ pāmpu kāṇappaṭṭatu pōlvatām. ‘cāntakat(tu) uṇ ṭeṉṟu cepput tiṟantoruvaṉ pāmpakattuk kaṇṭa tuṭittu.’ 	(nālaṭi. 126)
ikkaruttaminta pāṭalil pāmpi uvamam kūṟiyatu, accac cuvi payappatām.	(to. po. 294 pērā.)
acaṅkati aṇi -
ōriṭattuk kāraṇam iruppa, maṟṟōriṭattuk kāriyam nikaḻntatākak kūṟuvatu. itu cantirālōkattil toṭar piṉmiyaṇi eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ mūvakippaṭum eṉṟu viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu. taṇṭiyalaṅkārattil cittira ētuviṉ vakiyāṉa tūrakāriyētu eṉṉum peyarāl cuṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
‘tūra kāriya ētu’ kāṇka.	(mā. a. 203.)
acaṅkati cilēṭiyuṭaṉ varutal -
kāraṇam ōriṭattiruppak kāriyam vēṟōr iṭattu nikaḻtalik kūṟum acaṅkati aṇi cilēṭiyaṇiyoṭum kalantu varum.
e-ṭu :	maṉiḷaval viṇmēl maṟinta irāvaṇivaṉ
ceṉṉiyiṉmēl vāḷi ciṉat(tu) ēva - naṉṉutalāy!
ñālat(tu) acaṅkatiyē nāṭil irāvaṇaṉār
kālaṟṟa(tu) eṉṉum kati.
ilakkuvaṉ intiracittaṉ taliyi nōkki ampu eyya, irāvaṇaṉ kāl (cantati) aṟṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟa karuttaminta ip pāṭaluḷ, kāl eṉpatu uṟuppiṉik kuṟikkumpōtu acaṅkati aṇiyākavum vantuḷḷatu. irāvaṇaṉ taṉ putalvar mūvaruḷ aṭcakumāraṉ atikāyaṉ eṉṟa iruvari yum muṉṉē iḻantamiyāl, intiracittu iṟantataṉōṭu avaṉatu cantati aḻintu viṭṭatu eṉpatu.	(mā. a. 204)
acampavam (1) -
cērakkūṭāmi. ovvāmi kūṟum ilakkaṇam. iḵtu ilakkiyam oṉṟiṉum illiyākum kuṟṟam. (tarukka caṅ)
e-ṭu :	“pacu oṟṟik kuḷampuṭiyatu”eṉṟal
acampavam (2) -
itu māṇikkavācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (69) varuvatōr aṇi.
nikaḻa iyalāta payaṉ varum karutti nikaḻa iyalāta nikaḻcci yākak kūṟuvatu. itu kūṭāmi aṇiyām.
e-ṭu :	kuruṭu kāṇṭal pakalum illi.
acampavālaṅkāram -
kūṭāmiyaṇi (aṇi. 36) atu kāṇka.
acātāraṇa upamā - 
potunīṅku uvami; atu kāṇka.
aṭuttu varaluvami -
‘uvamattiṟku uvamam kūṟutal kūṭāmi’ - kāṇka.
uvamik kuvami (cīvaka. 107 uri)
aṭipotuvāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭa  varum oṭṭaṇi -
oṭṭaṇivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟu. kavi tāṉ kūṟakkarutiya poruḷi (- upamēyatti) maṟittup piṟitoṉṟaṉi (upamāṉatti)k kūṟi apporuḷik kuṟippāl uṇarttumvaki. iraṇṭaṟkum potuvāṉa aṭimoḻi kaḷi amippatu itaṉ ilakkaṇam.
‘uḷnilavu nīrmittāy, ōvāp payaṉcurantu,
taṇṇaḷi tāṅku malarmukattuk - kaṇṇekiḻntu
nīṅkal ariya niḻaluṭittāy niṉṟemakkē
ōṅkiyatōr cōli uḷatu’
ippāṭaṟkaṇ, vaḷḷal oruvaṉ cōliyākak kāṭṭap paṭukiṟāṉ. cōliyākiya uvamiyiṉ āṟṟalāl vaḷḷal ākiya karutiya poruḷ kuṟippāl uṇarttappaṭukiṟatu.
1) cōli naṭuvē nīrniliyip peṟṟuḷatu; 2) māṟāta payaṉ kaḷit taruvatu; 3) vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum pūkkaḷ malarap peṟṟatu; 4) appūkkaḷiṭi  matu nekiḻappeṟṟatu; 5) taṉpāl tarukkaḷiṉ niḻal nīṅkappeṟātatu. cōliyiṉ avvaṭimoḻi aṉittum kuṟippāl uṇarttappeṟum vaḷḷaliṭattum amivaṉa.
1) vaḷḷal taṉṉuḷattē iṉimip paṇpuṭiyāṉ;  2) aṉivarkkum nīṅkāta payaṉ palavum curakkiṟāṉ; 3) karuṇi kūrnta malarnta mukamuṭiyāṉ; 4) ammukattē kaṇṇōṭṭam kāṭṭukiṟāṉ; 5) taṉṉi piritaṟkariya pātukāppām niḻal tantu purakkiṟāṉ.
ivvāṟu veḷippaṭiyāṉa uvamiyiṉ aṭimoḻi yellām, kuṟippāl uṇarttappaṭum poruḷukkum potuvāka amintaṉa.	(taṇṭi. 53-2)
aṭimāṉam -
uvami (yāḻ. aka.)	(ṭu)
aṭiyaṭuttuvanta uvami uruvakam -
aṭiyaṭuttu vanta upamāṉatti upamēyattiṟkup poruttip piṉ upamēyatti uruvakappaṭuttum uruvaka vaki.
e-ṭu :	‘miyēr kuvaḷikkaṇ vaṇṭiṉam vāḻum’
 kuvaḷiyip pōṉṟa kaṇ - uvami
 kuvaḷi - upamāṉam; kaṇ - upamēyam
 kaṇṇākiya vaṇṭiṉam - uruvakam.
kuvaḷikku ‘miyēr kuvaḷi’ eṉṟu aṭi koṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. paṭavē, ‘miyēr kuvaḷikkaṇ’ eṉpatu aṭiyaṭuttu vanta uvami. ‘miyēr kuvaḷikkaṇ vaṇṭiṉam’ eṉpatu aṭiyaṭuttu vanta uvami uruvakam.			(mā. a. pā. 259 uri)
aṭiyum poruḷum ayalpaṭa vanta oṭṭaṇi -
taṇṭiyalaṅkāram kuṟippiṭum oṭṭaṇivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟu. aḵtāvatu pāṭaliluḷḷa aṭimoḻikaḷum avvaṭimoḻikaḷiyuṭiya upamāṉamum, avaṟṟiṉ  vēṟupaṭṭa aṭimoḻikaḷi yuṭiya upamēyattik kuṟippāl uṇartta varuvatu.
e-ṭu :	‘veṟikoḷ iṉaccurumpu mēyntatōr kāvik
 kuṟipaṭutēṉ vēṭṭum kuṟukum - niṟimatucērn (tu)
 uṇṭāṭum taṇmukattē cevvi yuṭiyatōr
 vaṇṭā maripirinta vaṇṭu.’
itaṉkaṇ, tēṉ nirampi aḻakiya cevviyi yuṭiya vaḷaviya tāmariyip pirinta vaṇṭu, palavaṇṭukaḷum tōyntu tēṉ uṇṭatāl tēṉ vaṟṟiya kuvaḷiyi nāṭic ceṉṟu ataṉ tēṉip parukukiṟatu eṉṟa upamāṉam, kuṟippiṉāl, kāmaccevvi nukarum paruvattāḷākiya aḻakiya taliviyip pirintu, talivaṉ, palarum nukarnta kāmac cevvi kulinta parattiyiṉ iṉpam nāṭic ceṉṟatākiya upamēyatti aṟivuṟukkiṟatu.
ippāṭaliluḷḷa aṭiyum poruḷum upamāṉattiṟkē uriyaṉavāy upamēyattiṟku uriyaṉa alla ātaliṉ, iḵtu aṭiyum poruḷum ayalpaṭavanta oṭṭaṇi vakippaṭum.
	(taṇṭi. 53 - 1)
aṭiyi viparītappaṭuttup poruḷ vēṟupaṭa moḻiyum oṭṭaṇi -
veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟappaṭum upamāṉattiṟkum kuṟippāl peṟappaṭum upamēyattiṟkum aṭimoḻikaḷi māṟupaṭa amittuc ceyyum iḵtu oṭṭaṇivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟu.
e-ṭu :	‘kaṭikol ulakiyaṟki! kālattiṉ tīṅkāl;
 aṭiya vaṟitāyiṟ(ṟu). aṉṟē - aṭivōrk(ku) 
 arumi yuṭittaṉṟi antēṉ cuvittāyk
 karumi viravāk kaṭal’
“taṉṉi vantaṭivōrkku eytutaṟku ariyatu ākāmal (eḷitē kiṭippatāy), iṉcoṟkaḷiyuṭiyatāy, karumi eṉpatē illāta kaṭalāṉatu, kālattiṉ tīmiyāl, yām ceṉṟaṭintapōtu vaṟṟiviṭṭatē!” eṉṟa karut tuṭiya ippāṭal, koṭiyāl vaṟumi eytiviṭṭa vaḷḷal oruvaṉic ceṉṟaṭinta iravalaṉ kūṟṟāka amintuḷḷatu.
kaṭal - upamāṉam; vaḷḷal - upamēyam. kaṭalukku aṭi yāvaṉa arumiyuṭiyatātal, cuviyillāmi, karumi yuṭittātal eṉa ivi. ivaṟṟi viparītappaṭutti, upamēya māṉa vaḷḷalukku eḷitāṉa cevvi, iṉcolluṭimi, karumi viravāmi eṉa ākkiyuḷḷami ivvaṇivakiyām.		(taṇṭi. 53-4)
aṭi viravip poruḷ vēṟupaṭa varum oṭṭaṇi -
oṭṭaṇivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟu. kavi tāṉ kūṟak karutiya poruḷi (-upamēyatti) maṟittup piṟitoṉṟākiya uvamiyik kūṟik kuṟippāl apporuḷi uṇarttum vakiyil, iraṇṭaṟkum viravivarum aṭimoḻikaḷi amippatu itaṉ ilakkaṇam.
e-ṭu :	taṇṇaḷicērn(tu) iṉcol maruvum takimittāy,
eṇṇiya epporuḷum ennāḷum - maṇṇulakil
vantu, namakkaḷittu vāḻum mukiloṉṟu
tantatāl muṉṉit tavam!
ippāṭaṟkaṇ, vaḷḷal oruvaṉ mukil eṉak kāṭṭap peṟukiṟāṉ. mukil - upamāṉam; vaḷḷal upamēyamākiya karutiya poruḷ. atu kuṟippāl īṇṭu uṇarttappaṭukiṟatu.
mukil taṇṇaḷi yuṭiyatu; vaḷḷalum karuṇi mikkavaṉ. ākavē ‘taṇṇaḷi cērntu’ eṉṉum aṭimoḻi uvami poruḷ iraṇṭaṟkum potuvāy viravivantatu. iṉcol maruvutal, eṇṇiya epporuḷum ennāḷum maṇṇulakil vantu aḷittal eṉṉum piṉ vanta iraṇṭu aṭimoḻikaḷum vaḷḷalākiya upamēyattiṟku māt tiramē cērvaṉa.
ivvāṟu veḷippaṭiyāṉa upamāṉattiṟkum, kuṟippāl pōtarum upamēyattiṟkum aṭimoḻikaḷ kalantu vantaṉa.	(taṇṭi. 53-3)
aṭivu aṇi
itu niralniṟi aṇi eṉavumpaṭum.
e-ṭu :	‘cevvāy pukarpunti tiṅkaṭ katirpunti
 cevvāypoṉ kāricaṉi mantri cevvāy - evvāyum
 mēṭamutal īrāṟu vīṭṭuk(ku) iṟiyeṉṟu
 nīṭāyntu coṉṉār nilattu.’
itu cevvāy mutaliya kōḷkaḷip paṉṉiraṇṭu irācikkum nāyakarāka aṭivē vittuc coṉṉami yāṉ, aṭivu aṇi āyiṟṟu. (katir - cūriyaṉ; punti - putaṉ; pukar - cukkiraṉ; poṉ - viyāḻaṉ; mantri - kuru; kāri - caṉi.)
mēṭam - cevvāy
iṭapam - cukkiraṉ
mituṉam - putaṉ
kaṭakam - cantiraṉ
cimmam - cūriyaṉ
kaṉṉi - putaṉ
tulām - cukkiraṉ
viruccikam - cevvāy
taṉucu - kuru
makaram - caṉi
kumpam - caṉi
mīṉam - kuru
mēṟkaṇṭa pāṭaluḷ iraṇṭāmaṭi citintuḷḷatu.
‘veḷḷicevvāy poṉcaṉi kāripoṉ’
eṉṟu pāṭam koṇṭāltāṉ poruḷ amiyum. 
	(vī. cō. 154 uri)
‘uyttuṇar niralniṟi’ itaṉiṉ vēṟupaṭutal kāṇka.
aṇi eṉṉum peyarkkāraṇam -
pulavarāl toṭukkappaṭṭa kavikaḷukkup poruḷāṉum collāṉum aḻaku eytap puṇarkkappaṭum poruḷ uṟuppu aṇi eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu.	(mā. a. 86)
orutti ellā āparaṇaṅkaḷum pūṇṭu niṉṟāḷēṉum, avaṟṟuḷ ciṟappuṭiyatu oṉṟaṉāl ‘āram pūṇṭu niṉṟāḷ’ eṉpatu pōla, oru ceyyuṭkaṇ pala aṇikaḷ iruppiṉum avvaṇikaḷuḷ mikkatoṉṟē ciṟappākak koḷḷappaṭum.	(vī. cō. 143 uri)
aṇi ceyyuḷi viḷakkutal -
akamum puṟamum ākiya poruḷ iraṇṭaṉukkum iṭam ceyyuḷ ātaliṉ, ceyyuḷi aḻakuṟutti ataṉ poruḷit teḷivuṟat  tōṟṟuvikkavē aṇikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉpa. eṉavē, aṇikaḷāvaṉa ceyyuṭ karutti vaṉappuṟa viḷakki niṟpaṉa.	(i. vi. 621)
aṇinta eṉṟa uvamurupu -
‘muliyaṇinta muṟuvalāḷ’ (kali. kaṭavuḷ.)
mullimuki pōṉṟa paṟkaḷiyuṭiyavaḷ eṉṟa poruḷuṭiya ittoṭaril, mulli paṟkaḷukku vaṭiva uvamamum niṟa uvamamum paṟṟi varukiṟatu. aṇinta eṉṉum uvama urupu mey, uru uvamam paṟṟi vantatu. (mey - vaṭivam; uru - niṟam)	(nac. uri)
aṇiyiyal tōṉṟiya muṟi -
tolkāppiyaṉār uvamam oṉṟaṉiyē aṇiyākak kūṟiyoḻintār. ēṉiya kuṇaaṇikaḷum, uvami oḻinta poruḷ aṇikaḷum, collaṇikaḷum perumpāṉmiyum tolkāppiyam mutaliya tamiḻnūlkaḷuḷḷum, ciṟu pāṉmi alaṅkāram mutaliya vaṭanūlkaḷuḷḷum col vēṟupāṭaṉṟip poruḷ vēṟupāṭu iṉmiyāṉum, cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkum vaṭamoḻic ceytikaḷip potu makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum avvannāṭṭu moḻikaḷil peyarttu vaḻaṅkutal muṟiyākalāṉum, vaṭa nūṟceytikaḷiyum koṇṭu aṇiyiyal amittaṟkaṇ iḻukku oṉṟum illi.	(i. vi. 635 uri)
(i. vi. poruḷatikārattuḷ aminta ōriyal aṇiyiyal)
aṇiyiyalil kūṟappaṭṭa ceytikaḷ -
muttakam kuḷakam tokinili toṭarnili eṉṟa ceyyuḷ tiṟaṉum, vitaruppam kuṭam pāñcālam eṉṟa neṟikaḷum, pattuk kuṇaaṇikaḷum, poruḷaṇikaḷ palavum, aṭimaṭakku colmaṭakku eḻuttumaṭakku eṉṟa mūvakimaṭakkum, cittirakavikaḷum, oṉpatuvaki vaḻukkaḷum, āṟuvaki malivukaḷum aṇiyiyalil kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.			(i. vi. 709)
aṇiyiyaluḷ ceyyuḷip paṟṟik kūṟum iyipu -
aṇi eṉṉum vaṉappiṉik kāṇpataṟkuriya caṭṭakam ceyyuḷ ātaliṉ, ceyyuḷ vakikaḷākiya pā iṉaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya potuppaṭiyāṉa ceytikaḷ aṇiyiyaluḷ kūṟap paṭal vēṇṭum. aviyaṉṟi, yāppilakkaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭum eḻuttu aci cīr aṭi toṭi mutaliyaṉa aṇiyiyaluḷ kūṟappeṟuvaṉa alla.	(mā. a. 66 uri)
aṇiyiyaluḷ vaḻuvum malivum kūṟavēṇṭiya
iṉṟiyamiyāmi -
ceyyuḷ eṉpaṉa caṭṭakam, alaṅkāram eṉa iraṇṭām. avaṟṟuḷ alaṅkāram eṉpaṉa accaṭṭakattip polivu ceyvaṉa ākalāṉ, ceyyuḷukkup polivuceyyum alaṅ kāraṅkaḷik kūṟumpōtē, polivu aḻivu ceyvaṉa vaṟṟiyum kūṟi, avi vārāta vakiyāl ceyyuḷi amittal vēṇṭum eṉpataṉiyum kūṟal vēṇṭumāta liṉ, ceyyuḷuḷ varutal kūṭātu eṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭa vaḻukkaḷum malivukaḷum aṇiyiyaluḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.		(mā. a. 306 uri) (i. vi. 691 uri)
aṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇam -
poruḷiyaliṟ kūṟiya akapporuḷ puṟapporuḷ eṉṉum iraṇṭaṉiyum kuṇam alaṅkāram eṉṉum iruvaki yāṉum cuvipaṭa viḷakki nikaḻvatu aṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇa mām.	(i. vi. aṇiyiyal 2)
pulavarāl toṭukkappaṭṭa kavikaṭkup poruḷāṉum collāṉum aḻaku eytap puṇarppatu aṇiyākum. poruḷāl toṭukkappaṭum aṇi aḻakākum; collāl toṭukkappaṭum aṇi pūṇākum.	(mā. a. 86 uri)
aṇivaki -
kuṇavaṇiyum poruḷaṇiyum collaṇiyum eṉa aṇi muttiṟappaṭum. iṉimiyum eḷimiyum meṉmiyum oḻukiciyum teḷivum kavarcciyum uyttalil poruṇ miyum ilakkaṇak kuṟippum ceṟivum mutalāṉa coṟ poruḷ naṭiyiṉik kuṇavaṇi eṉpar. pāṭṭiṉuḷ amiyum ikkuṇavaṇi tolkāppiyam cuṭṭum eṭṭu vaki vaṉappiṉpāṟpaṭum. taṉminaviṟci mutalāka pāvikam īṟākak kiṭanta muppatum poruḷnayatti pulappaṭuttum ciṟappuṭimiyāl poruḷaṇi eṉappaṭum. antāti iraṭṭi aḷapeṭi muraṇ eṉṉum toṭikaḷum niralniṟiyum tīpakamum piṉvaruniliyum coṟṟoṭar paṟṟiya poruḷaṇiyām. maṭakkaṇiyum cittirakaviyum piṟavum collaṇiyām. 	(teṉ. aṇi.2,3,47)
attac cilēṭi uvami -
poruḷ paṟṟiya cilēṭi vāypāṭu koṇṭu uvamikkum uvami vaki itu.
e-ṭu :	‘peṟṟāḷ ikaḻa vaḷarttāḷ iṭarceyap pērttuṟavoṉ(ṟu) 	 aṟ(ṟu)ākam eṅkum karukiyum vāyviṭ(ṭu) araṟṟumantō	 poṟṟā   marikkaṇṇaṉ viṇṇōr pirāṉpuṭ kuḻiyil vañcam
	kaṟṟār poruṭpiri(vu) uṟṟiṭi vēṉiṟ karuṅkuyilē.’
kaḷavu nīkki maṇamceytukōṭaṟkut talivaṉ poruḷ tēṭivarap piriya muṟpaṭṭapōtu, tōḻi avaṉ piriviṭit talivi paṭun tuyari oru kuyili viḷittu ataṉ tuyarik kūṟuvāḷ pōla, “peṟṟa tāy ikaḻa, vaḷartta tāy tuṉpuṟutta, uṟaviṉar oruvarum ātarippār ilarāka, uṭal muḻutum karuki, vāyviṭṭuk kataṟi varuntum kuyil pōla, kaḷavoḻukkam aṟintu naṟṟāy ikaḻa, iṟceṟittuc cevilittāy tuṉpuṟutta, uṟaviṉarkaḷ ēca, pirivut tuyarāl cemmēṉi kaṟuttup pōyt tuyaram tāṅkāmal talivi vāyviṭṭuk kataṟuvāḷ” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭatu ivvaṇi āmāṟu kāṇka.
kuyil taṉ muṭṭiyik kākkiyiṉ kūṭṭil vittuc cellum. muṭṭiyiliruntu pārpput tōṉṟik kuyil kuralil olittatum, kākki ataṉit taṉ kūṭṭiṉiṉṟu viraṭṭiviṭum. ituvē ‘peṟṟāḷ ikaḻa, vaḷarttāḷ iṭarceya’ eṉpatu.	(mā. a. pāṭal 214)
attiyantāpāvam -
attiyanta + apāvam; muḻutu miṉmi.	(pirapōta. 42-4) (ṭu)
atputarasam -
aṟputaccuvi. itu cuviyaṇi vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itu maruṭkic cuvi eṉavum paṭum. ‘viyappuccuvi aṇi’ kāṇka.
atputarūpakam -
tamiḻ nūlār itaṉi ‘viyappu uruvakam’ eṉpa. atu kāṇka.
atputōpamā -
tamiḻ nūlār itaṉi ‘viyappu uvami’ eṉpa. atu kāṇka.
atyukti alaṅkāram -
itaṉi ‘mikuti naviṟci aṇi’ eṉpa tamiḻ nūlār. atu kāṇka.
atatkuṇālaṅkāram -
itaṉit tamiḻnūlār ‘piṟitiṉkuṇam peṟāmiaṇi’ eṉpar. ataṉuḷ kāṇka.
atika aṇi -
tāṅkum poruḷiviṭat tāṅkappaṭum poruḷ uruvam peruttu, iṉi  vaḷartal kūṭātu eṉṟu aṭaṅkiyatu eṉak kūṟuvatu ivvaṇi.
e-ṭu :	‘vaciyilā iracatattiṉāl vayiṉvayiṉ amitta
 acivilā paṉivarikkulam aḷaviṭaṟ(ku) aritām
 ticikaḷ eṅkaṇumceṉ(ṟu) iṭampeṟātavām ataṉa(tu)
 icikaḷ niṉ(ṟu) avaṇceṟin(tu) aṭaṅkiya teṉalāmē’
veḷḷiyālamitta ceykuṉṟukaḷ ticikaḷeṅkum paravik kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ pukaḻ pūvulakam vāṉavulakam eṅkaṇum paravi iṉip paravutaṟku iṭamilli eṉṟu mēlum paravāmal niṉṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟa ippāṭaṟ karuttil atika aṇi amintuḷḷatu.	(mā. a. 178)
atikam (1) -
itu māṇikkavācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (73) varuvatōr aṇi.
upamāṉattiviṭa upamēyattiṟku uyarvu koḷvatu.
e-ṭu :	‘kuṭṭanīrk kuvaḷi yellām kūṭimuṉ niṟka māṭṭāk
 kaṭṭaḻa(ku) aminta kaṇṇāḷ’  (cīvaka. 710)
atikam (2) -
itu māṇikka vācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (77) varuvatōr aṇi.
ātārattiṉum ātēyam peritāka vaḷarntu iṭamiṉmi yiṉ ataṉōṭu niṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu. ‘atika aṇi kāṇka.’
atikālaṅkāram -
‘atika aṇi’ kāṇka.
aticaya aṇi -
aticayamāvatu mikuti. kavi tāṉ karutiya poruḷiṉatu vaṉappiṉi uyarttic colluṅkāl, ulakanaṭi varampu kaṭavāmal, uyarntōr viyakkum vakiyil colluvatu. 			(taṇṭi. 54)
iḵtu uyarvu naviṟci, mikimoḻi, perukku eṉṉum peyar kaḷiyum peṟum. uriya poruḷi uyarttic collum utātta aṇiyākiya vīṟukōḷaṇiyiṉ itu vēṟām.
itu poruḷaticayam, kuṇaaticayam, toḻilaticayam, aiyaaticayam, tuṇivuaticayam, tiripuaticayam eṉa āṟu vakippaṭum.	(taṇṭi. 55)
iṭam, ciṉi, kālam ivaṟṟiyum mā. a. (145) kuṟikkum. kāraṇa mikimoḻi, akāraṇa mikimoḻi eṉṉum pakuppukkaḷi vī.cō. (177) kuṟikkum. ippākupāṭukaḷi avvattalippukkaḷil kāṇka.
aticaya aṇiyiṉ maṟupeyarkaḷ -
1. perukku aṇi (vī. cō. 153), 2. mikimoḻi aṇi (vī. cō. 153 uri), 3. uyarvu naviṟci aṇi (ca. 28, kuva. 13) eṉpaṉa.
aticaya uruvakam -
itu māṇikkavācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (30) varuvatōr aṇi.
upamāṉattil upamēyattiṉ ceytiyik kuṟippiṭuvatu.
e-ṭu :	‘kuḻiaruku tāḻak kuṉipuruvam tāṅki
 uḻiyar uyirparuki nīṇṭa - viḻiyuṭittāy
 vaṇ(ṭu)ē(ṟu) iruḷ aḷakam cūḻnta mukamatiyāḷ
 koṇṭāḷeṉ uḷḷam kuṟittu’.   (taṇṭi. 33-1)
ippāṭalil, upamēyamāṉa mukattiṉ ceyti upamāṉamāṉa matikku ēṟṟi urikkappaṭṭavāṟu.
aticaya uvami -
uvami vakikaḷuḷ aticaya uvami oṉṟu. uyarvu naviṟci yāka upamāṉatti upamēyattuṭaṉ iṇittuk kūṟum uvami vaki itu.
e-ṭu :	‘niṉṉuḻiyē niṉmukam kāṇṭum, neṭuntaṭam
 taṉṉuḻiyē taṉṉiyum kāṇkuvam - eṉṉum
 ituoṉṟu mēyaṉṟi vēṟṟumimaṟ ṟuṇṭō,
 matuoṉṟu centā marikku?’
itaṉkaṇ, “uṉṉiṭattil uṉmukam uḷḷatu; tāmari taṭākattil uḷḷatu. inta vēṟupāṭaṉṟi, ivaṟṟiṭiyē piṟa vēṟupāṭu illiyē!” eṉat talivaṉ taliviyatu mukattic centāmariyē eṉa uyarttik kūṟutalāṉ, aticayaaṇi uvamiyil payiṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.(taṇṭi. 33-3)
aticaya palam -
itu māṇikkavācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (33) varuvatōr aṇi.
kāraṇamum kāriyamum tōṉṟiya piṉṉarē vara vēṇṭiya payaṉ, avi tōṉṟutaṟku muṉṉar varutal.
e-ṭu :	talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ piritalākiya kāraṇamum, talivi tuyar mikku varuntum kāriyamum ākiya ivi nikaḻum muṉpē, avaḷ taṉ mārpil taḻuvi vanta putalvaṉ taliyilaṇinta pūkkaḷit taṉ perumūcciṉāl karukac ceytal pōlvaṉa. 			(akanā. 5)
talivaṉpirivākiya kāraṇamum talivituyarākiya kāriya mum tōṉṟu muṉṉarē, avaḷukku veppamāṉa perumūccu vantatu eṉpatām.
aticaya pētakam -
itu māṇikkavācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (109) varuvatōr aṇi.
oru poruḷi uvamiyiṉṟi uyarcciyuṭiyatākak kūṟuvatu.
e-ṭu :	kōpuram vāṉaḷāvi niṟkiṟatu eṉṟal pōlvaṉa.
aticayam -
itu māṇikka vācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (31) varuvatōr aṇi.
oru poruḷiṉkaṇ uḷḷa ciṟappiṉi maṟṟoru poruḷil uḷḷatāka ēṟṟit talitaṭumāṟṟamākak kūṟuvatu.
e-ṭu :	‘mātar mukampōl oḷiviṭa valliyēl
 kātali vāḻi mati’   (kuṟaḷ. 1118)
ippāṭalil cantiraṉuṭiya oḷi mātar mukattukku ēṟṟi mikuttu urikkappaṭṭavāṟu.
aticaya yōkam -
itu māṇikkavācakar kuvalayāṉantattuḷ aṇiyiyalil (32) varuvatōr aṇi.
kūṭāta poruḷik kūṭṭiyurippatu.
e-ṭu :	‘cantaṉattil centaḻalum taṇmatiyil vevviṭamum
vantaṉavē pōlumāl nummāṟṟam’   (taṇṭi. 32-22)
cantaṉattil veppamum, amutamayamāṉa cantiraṉil viṭamum kūṭṭiyurittal kūṭāta poruḷkaḷik kūṭṭi yuritta vāṟām. itu kūṭā uvamikkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
aticayālaṅkāram -
aticayavalaṅkāram eṉṉum māṟaṉ alaṅkāram. (mā.a. 143,144) aticaya aṇi kāṇka.
aticayōkti -
ivvaṇi uyarvunaviṟci eṉavum, aticayaaṇi eṉavumpaṭum. ‘aticayaaṇi’ kāṇka.
aticayōpamā -
itu miki uvami eṉavum, aticaya uvami eṉavumpaṭum. ‘aticaya uvami’ kāṇka.
anta tīpakālaṅkāram -
tīvaka aṇivakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. kaṭinili viḷakkaṇi eṉavum paṭum. kuṇam toḻil cāti poruḷ ivi kuṟittuc ceyyuḷil ōriṭattu niṉṟa col piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum ceṉṟu iyintu poruḷ taruvatu tīvaka aṇi. ceyyuḷiṉ iṟutiyil niṉṟa col ēṉiya mutal iṭikaḷilum ceṉṟu poruḷpayappatu kaṭinilit tīvakam ākiya antatīpakālaṅkāram.
e-ṭu :	‘tuṟavuḷavāc cāṉṟōr iḷivaravum, tūya
 piṟavuḷavā ūṉtuṟavā ūṇum - paṟikaṟaṅkak
 koṇṭāṉ iruppak koṭuṅkuḻiyāḷ teyvamum
 uṇṭāka vikkaṟpāṟ ṟaṉṟu’
itaṉkaṇ, ‘uṇṭāka vikkaṟpāṟṟaṉṟu’ eṉṉum iṟutik kaṇ niṉṟa toṭar iḷivaravu, ūṇ, teyvam eṉap piṟa iṭattum ceṉṟu poruḷ payantavāṟu. toḻil kuṟittu vanta anta tīpakālaṅkāram itu.	(taṇṭi. 40- 10)
antāti uvami -
oru ceyyuḷakattup pala poruḷukkup pala uvami vantāl, mutalil uvamam ceyta upamēyap poruṭpeyariṉi ākupeyarākavāvatu aṉmoḻittokiyākavāvatu pirittu niṟuttip piṉ avvupamēyap peyariṉi antātittu upamāṉamākki uvamurupu koṭuttup piṉ upamēyat tōṭu iṇippatu antāti uvamiyām.
īrntunilam tōyum irumpiṭit taṭakkiyiṉ
cērntuṭaṉ ceṟinta kuṟaṅkiṉ, kuṟaṅkeṉa
mālvari oḻukiya vāḻi, vāḻip
pūveṉap polinta ōti  (ciṟupāṇ. 19-22)
peṇyāṉiyiṉ tutikkiyiṉip pōlak kālkaḷoṭu toṭarpupaṭṭu muṟiyākap paruttu oṉṟōṭoṉṟu neruṅki yirukkum toṭiyiṉiyuṭiyāḷ; toṭiyip pōla malikaḷil vāḻikaḷ vaḻuvaḻuppuṭiyavāy amintuḷa; avvāḻiyiṉ pūvip pōṉṟa vaṭivattil muṭiyappaṭṭa mayir muṭiyiṉi yuṭiyāḷ - eṉpataṉkaṇ, ‘cērntuṭaṉ ceṟinta kuṟaṅkiṉ’ eṉpataṉik kuṟaṅkiṉi yuṭiyāḷ eṉa aṉmoḻit tokiyākkip pirittup piṉ kuṟaṅku eṉpataṉi vāḻikku uvamiyākak koḷḷu talum avvāḻiyiṉ pūviṉi, mayirmuṭikku uvamiyākak koḷḷutalum poruntumāṟu, ‘kuṟaṅkiṉ kuṟaṅkeṉa’ eṉavum, ‘vāḻi vāḻippūveṉa’ eṉavum antātiyāka varutaliṉ antāti uvamiyām. (nūluḷ vēṟutāraṇam kāṭṭappeṟum.)	(mā. a. 101 - 24)
antiya kuḷakam -
aintu pāṭalkaḷum aintaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷum toṭarntu, viṉi viṉikkuṟippu peyar toḻiṟpeyar ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉi iṟutippāṭaṟkaṇ muṭikkum col lākak koṇṭu poruḷ muṟṟuppeṟumāyiṉ antiya kuḷakam eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu :	‘cempiṉi urukki vākkit tiṇṇitiṉ akaḻi tūrttiṭ(ṭu)
 umpariṉ mutalē veṇpoṉ uvāṉama taminta piṉṟip
 pimpoṉār cekati kaṇṭam paṭṭiki vakuttiṭ ṭōrcār
 imparniṉ ṟēṟa yāḷic curuṭpaṭi yiyaṟṟi maṉṉō,
‘pāṅkarvē tikikkut tāṉam pakuttaraṅ kiṉukku nāppaṇ
ōṅkupoṟ cuvarni ṟīivit turumacā ḷaramcey tōvā
vīṅkiruḷ tuṇikkum ceyya viḻumaṇi ataṉāl mēlvāyt
tāṅkumut taramvit tuḷvac cirattulām takaviṉ vittē,
	‘paccinaṉ maṇiyi īrnta palakimēṟ parappi nīlat
taccuṟu koṭuṅki mūṭṭi yataṉpuḻi akatta vāka
vaccira vaḷika ḷōṭi maṇiceyvē tikimāṇ tūṇat(tu)
uccimēṟ palaki ciṟṟut tarattiṉō ṭoṉṟac cērttē’
‘veḷḷiveṇ  palaki nāṉku viralviyaṉ nīppiṉ ṟāka
aḷḷuṟa nirittu vaṉpoṉ āṇikaḷ aḻuttik kiyāl
aḷḷupu tiraḷum oṇpoṉ arataṉam ñāṅkar mēyntu
teḷḷitiṉ ēḻatākat tikaḻnilam puṇarttu mātō,
	‘vārinīḷ niliyum mēlkīḻ marīiyanōṉ paṭiyum tāḷum
kūrkapā ṭamumoṉ ṟākak koḷīyamāṇ maṇikaḷ tammāl
cīrkeḻu tacumpu cūṭṭum cimiyamō ṭuyarntu vāṉuḷ
ārtamak kaminta taccark kaṇaṅkucey vaṉapoṉ māṭam.’
	(mā.a. pāṭalkaḷ 49- 53.)
ivi aintupāṭalkaḷum poruḷtoṭarpuṟa amintu, aintām pāṭal īṟṟaṭiyiṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa ‘poṉmāṭam’ eṉṟa peyarik koṇṭu muṭintaṉa.	(mā. a. 68 uri)
antiya tīpakam -
itu kaṭinilit tīvaka aṇi eṉavum, kaṭinili viḷak kaṇi eṉavum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkappeṟum. anta tīpakālaṅ kāramum atu. ‘kaṭinilit tīvaka aṇi’ kāṇka.
anniya uvami -
uvamiyākak kūṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṭattuḷḷa kuṟipāṭu kaḷic cuṭṭi, upamēyattiṟku upamāṉam yātu eṉṟu aṟiya iyalāta niliyik kuṟippatu anniya uvami. iḵtu, ‘iv viyalataṉāl ituvaṉṟu itu eṉac, cevvitiṉ teḷinta tēṟṟa uvami’yiṉ ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭatu. tēṟṟa uvami upamēyattiṟku upamāṉam  iṉṟu eṉa muṭivu ceyvatu. iḵtu akkarutti veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟātu viṭuppatu. iḵtu iraṇṭaṟkumiṭi vēṟṟumi.
 ‘kaṟpakampōl okkum kaṇuilli; kāvēri
 maṟpuṉalpō lāmorukāl vaṟṟātu - naṟpukaḻcēr
 nantiki maṉṉu pulavōr navitīrak
 kantakōṉ vantutavum ki’
pukaḻmikka amarāvati nakariyotta taṉṉūr aṭiyum pulavartam vaṟumitīrak kantaṉ eṉṟa vaḷḷal aḷḷi vaḻaṅkum kikaḷik kaṟpakam eṉṟu kūṟalāmeṉiṉ, kaṟpaka marattil uḷḷa kaṇukkaḷ kikaḷil illi; kāvēriyi uvami kūṟalām eṉil, kāvēri orukāl vaṟṟiviṭum, kiyiṉ koṭi vaṟṟātu. yātaṉi uvami kūṟuvatu eṉa itaṉkaṇ anniya uvami vantavāṟu.
anyōnyālaṅkāram -
tamiḻ aṇiyilakkaṇa nūlār ‘oṉṟaṟku oṉṟu utavi aṇi’ eṉpar. oṉṟu oṉṟaṟku upakarittalic collum alaṅkāram itu.
e-ṭu :	‘tiṅkaḷ ira vālviḷaṅkum; ceppukatirt tiṅkaḷāl
 kaṅkul viḷaṅkumē kāṇ.’  (kuva. 43)
itaṉkaṇ, tiṅkaḷ iraviṟku upakāra māyiruttalum avvāṟē iravum tiṅkaṭku upakāramāyiruttalum kūṟap paṭṭavāṟu.
anyōnyōpamā -
itaṉit tamiḻnūlār taṭumāṟuvami eṉpar. iḵtu orēpāṭalil aṭuttaṭuttu upamēyatti upamāṉamākavum, upamāṉatti upamēyamākavum māṟṟik kūṟuvatu. itaṉit taṇṭi alaṅkāram itaravitaruvami eṉṟum, māṟaṉa laṅkāram taṭumāṟuvamam eṉṟum, vīracōḻiyam uṟaḻntu varaluvami eṉṟum kūṟum.
e-ṭu :	‘kaḷikkum kayalpōlum niṉkaṇ; niṉ kaṇpōl
 kaḷikkum kayalum; kaṉivāyt - taḷirkkoṭiyē!
 tāmari pōlmalarum niṉmukam; niṉmukampōl
 tāmariyum cevvi tarum.’
kayal upamāṉam, kaṇ upamēyam;
kaṇ upamāṉam; kayal upamēyam;
tāmari upamāṉam; mukam upamēyam;
mukam upamāṉam, tāmari upamēyam -
eṉṟu ippāṭalil upamāṉamum upamēyamum taṭumāṟi vantuḷḷaṉa. anyōnyam, itarētaram, taṭumāṟṟam.... oru poruḷaṉa.	(taṇṭi 32-3)
ananyapāvōpamā -
itaṉit taṇṭiyāciriyar potu nīṅku uvami eṉpar. aḵtāvatu uvamiyik kūṟi maṟuttup poruḷtaṉṉiyē (upamēyatti) uvamiyākak kūṟuvatu.
e-ṭu :	‘tirumaruvu taṇmatikkum centā mariyiṉ
 virimalarkkum mēlām takittāl; - karuneṭuṅkaṇ
 māṉē! iruḷaḷakam cūḻntaniṉ vāṇmukam
 tāṉē uvami taṉakku’
itaṉkaṇ, taliviyatu mukam, uvamiyākiya taṇmati centāmari iviyiraṇṭiṉum mēlām takiyatu ātaliṉ, taṉakku uvamiyāvatu tāṉēyām eṉak kūṟappaṭṭami kāṇka. (aṇiyilakkaṇa nūlār iyipiṉmi aṇi eṉa itaṉic cuṭṭuvar.)	(taṇṭi 32- 23)
ananvayālaṅkāram -
itaṉit tamiḻ aṇiyilakkaṇa nūlār iyipiṉmi aṇi eṉpa. ananyapāvōpamā eṉpatum ivvaṇi. at talippuk kāṇka.	(kuva. 2)
anātarānkṣapam -
itu viruppiṉmi kūṟum ikaḻcci vilakku aṇi. muṉṉa vilakkaṇi vakiyuḷ oṉṟu. ‘ikaḻcci vilakku’ kāṇka.
aniyama uvami -
aniyamamāvatu variyaṟiyiṉmi. variyaṟi ceytu kūṟiya uvami oṉṟē āvataṉṟi, maṟṟu oṉṟum uvamiyām eṉpatu itu.
e-ṭu :	‘kuvi viritirinīrk kāviricūḻ naṉṉāṭṭu
 muval kamaḻum kuḻalmaṭavāy - cevvi
 matuvār kavirēniṉ vāypōlva taṉṟi
 atupōlva tuṇṭeṉiṉum ām.’
itaṉkaṇ, “peṇṇē! uṉatu vāykku muṇmuruṅkip pūvē uvamiyām eṉpatilli; atupōlvatu vēṟu ētēṉum uṇṭeṉiṉum uvamiyātal takum” eṉṟaṟkaṇ aniyama uvami vantavāṟu.	(taṇṭi. 32-10, mā.a. 101-15)
aniyamac cilēṭi -
niyama vilakkuc cilēṭi (taṇṭi. 78); atu kāṇka. 			(mā. a. 154)
aniyamōpamā -
itaṉi aniyama uvami eṉa māṟaṉalaṅkāram, taṇṭi ivi kūṟum. ‘aniyama uvami’ kāṇka.
anukñālaṅkāram -
itaṉit tamiḻ aṇiyilakkaṇa nūlār ‘vēṇṭal aṇi’ yeṉpa.
aḵtāvatu kuṟṟattāl kuṇam uṇṭāvatu kaṇṭu akkuṟṟatti vēṇṭutal.
e-ṭu :	‘veṇṭiru nīṟu puṉiyumā tavarkku
      viruntucey tuṟuperu miṭiyum
 koṇṭanal viratat tiḷikkumyāk kiyumnī
      koṭiyaṉēṟ karuḷunāḷ uḷatō?’
itaṉkaṇ, civaṉaṭiyārkku viruntūṭṭuvatāl varum vaṟumi, viratam pūṇuvatāl uṭampu taḷartal ākiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ maṟumiyiṉpam payakkum kuṇampaṟṟit taṉakku vēṇṭum eṉak kaviñaṉāl vēṇṭappaṭṭavāṟu kāṇka.			(kuva. 71)
anukñānkṣapam -
itaṉit taṇṭiyāciriyar muṉṉavilakkaṇiyuḷ oṉṟākiya ‘uṭaṉpaṭal vilakku’ eṉpa. atu kāṇka.
anukrōcānkṣapam -
muṉṉa vilakkaṇi vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya ‘iraṅkal vilakku’ eṉṟu taṇṭiyāciriyar itaṉik kuṟippiṭuvar. atu kāṇka.
anukuṇālaṅkāram -
itaṉit tamiḻ aṇinūlār taṉkuṇamikiyaṇi eṉpar. aḵtāvatu maṟṟoṉṟiṉ cārpiṉāl taṉatu kuṇam mikutalic colvatu.
e-ṭu :	‘vārcevicēr kāvimalar māṉaṉiyāy! niṉkaṭikkaṭ
 pārviyiṉāl mikkakarum paṇpu.’
itaṉkaṇ, taliviyatu kaṭikkaṭ pārviyatu cārpiṉāl kuvaḷimalarkkuk karumimikutal collappaṭṭatu. 			(kuva. 78)
anucayat taṭimoḻi -
itaṉi vilakkaṇi vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya ‘kiyaṟal vilakku’ eṉat taṇṭiyalaṅkāram kuṟikkum. atu kāṇka. 			(vī.cō. 163)
anupalatti aṇi -
itu tamiḻil nukarcciyiṉmi aṇi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappeṟum. atu nōkkuka. 	(kuva. 113)
anumāṉap piramāṇaaṇi -
anumāṉam - oru kāraṇamkoṇṭu kāriyatti uyt tuṇartal. ‘aṉumāṉam’ eṉpatu kāṇka. niṟuva vēṇṭiya poruḷiyum ataṉ ētuviyum aḻaku tōṉṟak kūṟuvatu ivvaṇi. iḵtu uruvakam, uyarvunaviṟci eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa aṇikaḷ kalantum varappeṟum.
1) cītiyip pirintu tuyaruṟukiṉṟa irāmaṉ nilavi veyileṉa mayaṅki “ilakkuva! maraniḻalukkuc celvōm; katiravaṉ mikavum kāykiṟāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟa, ilakkuvaṉ, “aṇṇāl! itu nilavu, cantiraṉnilavaṉṟō ītu!” eṉṟāṉ. “uṉakku eppaṭit teriyum, muḻunilavutāṉ eṉṟu?” eṉṟu irāmaṉ viṉavavē, ilakkuvaṉ, “cantiraṉ naṭuvē māṉ irukkiṟatu” eṉṟāṉ. māṉ eṉṟa coṟ kēṭṭatum, irāmaṉukku māṉ pōṉṟa kaṇṇiyām cītiyatu niṉivu mikkut tuyaramum mikkatu. “māṉ pōlum viḻiyum matipōlum mukamum paṭitta eṉ uyiraṉiyāy! cītiyē! nī yāṇṭuḷāy” eṉṟu avaṉ pulampaluṟṟāṉ.
itaṉkaṇ, uyarvu naviṟciyum mayakka aṇiyum niṉivaṇiyum kalantu, “katiravaṉ allaṉ, cantiraṉē” eṉṟu niṟuvutaṟkāṉa ētukkaḷāy, iravāy iruttalum mati naṭuvē māṉuruvam teritalum kūṟappaṭṭami kāṇka.
2) piṟa aṇi oṉṟum kalavāmal, ētuvum kāriyamumāyk kūṟappaṭum tūya anumāṉappiramāṇaaṇiyum uṇṭu.
“tattam kūṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu oṭuṅkum paṟavikaḷik koṇṭum, kūmpi mūṭukiṉṟa tāmarikaḷik koṇṭum, malliki malarvatikkoṇṭum, cūriyaṉ maṟintaṉaṉ eṉpatu pulaṉām” eṉṟu kūṟuvatu tūya anumāṉap piramāṇamākum.	(kuva. 109)
anēkāṅka uruvakam -
anēka aṅkam - paluṟuppu. oru poruḷiṉ uṟuppukkaḷil palavaṟṟiyum uruvakam ceyvatu anēkāṅka uruvaka aṇiyām.
e-ṭu :	‘kittaḷirāl koṅki mukiḻtāṅkik kaṇeṉṉum
 mittaṭañcēl mintar maṉam kalaṅka - vittatōr
 miṉuḷatāl mēka miciyuḷatāl maṟṟatuvum
 eṉuḷatām naṇpā! iṉi!’
talivaṉ pāṅkaṟku uṟṟaturikkum ikkiḷavippāṭaṟkaṇ “ki ākiya taḷirāl koṅkiyākiya moṭṭiṉit tāṅkik kaṇṇākiya mīṉ kāraṇamāka iḷiñar maṉam kalaṅka, mēlē mēkattit tāṅkiyuḷḷa miṉṉal oṉṟu eṉatu uḷattē uḷḷatu” eṉa, kiyit taḷirākavum, koṅkiyi mukiḻākavum, kaṇṇiṉi mīṉākavum, kūntali mēkamākavum, ivvāṟu uṟuppukkaḷi uruvakam ceytu uṟuppiyākiya taliviyiyum miṉṉalāka uruvakittami kāṇka.
avayavam, avayavi eṉa iraṇṭiyum uruvakippatu ivvaṇi. avayavaṅkaḷi māttiram uruvakam ceytu avayaviyi uruvakiyātu viṭuvatu avayava uruvakamām.			(taṇṭi. 37 - 12)
aprastuta pracamsālaṅkāram -
itaṉi tamiḻ nūlār ‘puṉivilip pukaḻcciyaṇi’ eṉpar. ‘māṟupaṭu pukaḻniliyaṇi’yum atu. viriviṉi avaṟṟuḷ kāṇka.
apahanuti -
itaṉit tamiḻ aṇinūlār avanuti eṉavum, oḻippaṇi eṉavum (aṇi -11) kūṟuvar. virivu avaṟṟuḷ kāṇka.			(taṇṭi. 23)
apāva ētu -
apāvam -iṉmi; ētu - kāraṇam. iṉmi eṉṉum apāvattiyum ētu aṇiyiṉpāṟpaṭuttuk kūṟupa. oṉṟiṉ iṉmiyik kāraṇamkāṭṭik kūṟal itaṉ ilakkaṇam. iḵtu aivakippaṭum.
‘yāṇṭu moḻitiṟampār cāṉṟavar; emmaruṅkum
īṇṭu mukilkaḷ iṉamiṉamāy - mūṇṭeḻunta 
kāliyē kārmuḻaṅkum eṉṟayarēl; kātalartēr 
māliyē nampāl varum.’
“‘ellā maruṅkiṉiṉṟum mēkaṅkaḷ neruṅkik kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāy vantavuṭaṉēyē muḻaṅkik kārkālatti aṟivikkiṉṟaṉavē’ eṉṟa nī varuntaṟka. cāṉṟōr coṉṉa col tavaṟutal eññāṉṟum illi. talivaṉ cāṉ ṟōṉ ākaliṉ, kuṟitta paruvattē vantuviṭuvāṉ. avaṉ tēr iṉṟumāliyē nammiṭam varum” eṉṟu paruvaṅkaṇṭu varuntiya talivikkut tōḻi āṟutal kūṟukiṟāḷ.
cāṉṟōr col tavaṟār eṉṉum kāraṇam, talivaṉ tēr tavaṟātu varum eṉpataṟku ētuvāyiṉamiyiṉ apāva ētuvām.	(taṇṭi. 61, 62-1)
apāvētu vaki -
eṉṟum apāvam, iṉmiyatu apāvam, oṉṟiṉ oṉṟu apāvam, uḷḷataṉ apāvam, aḻivupāṭṭapāvam eṉa apāva ētuaṇi aivakippaṭum.	(taṇṭi. 62)
apūta uvami -
apūtam - ulakil illāta poruḷ. ulakil illāta poruḷi upamāṉam ākki urikkum ivvaṇi uvami vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ilporuḷ uvami eṉavum itu vaḻaṅkappeṟum.
e-ṭu :	‘ellāk kamalat teḻilum tiraṇṭoṉṟiṉ
 villēr puruvattu vēlneṭuṅkaṇ - nallīr!
 mukampōlum eṉṉa, muṟuvalittār vāḻum
 akampōlum eṅkaḷ akam.’
ulakattē yuḷḷa tāmarippūkkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉ aḻakiyum orucērat tokuttāl, avvaḻaku taliviyatu mukattaḻaku pōlām eṉṟa karuttiṉkaṇ, avvāṟu tokuttalākiya illāta nikaḻcciyit talivi mukattaḻakiṟku uvami yākkiyamiyāl apūta uvami nikaḻntavāṟu.(taṇṭi. 32- 19)
apūtōpamā -
aḵtāvatu apūta uvami; ilporuḷ uvami eṉavum paṭum. ‘apūta uvami’ kāṇka.
apētattip pētamākkiya aṟputa uvami
vēṟupaṭāta toṉṟaṉi vēṟupaṭutti aṟputa vāy - pāṭṭāl kūṟum uvamivaki itu.
‘mēlirunta viṇṇōr viyattakka cemmiyatāyp
pālikiyuḷ teḷḷamutam pālikkum - cālpuṭiya
tiṅkaḷikkaṇ ṭālmāṟaṉ cempoṉ tuṭariyuḷyār
taṅkaḷuḷaṅ kōṭā tavar’
cantiraṉ oṉṟaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟu illiyākavum, ‘cem miyi yuṭiyatāyp pālikiyuḷ teḷinta amutatti veḷippaṭuttum cantiraṉ’ eṉat talivimukamākiya cantiraṉi iyaṟkiyāṉa cantiraṉiṭattiṉiṉṟu vēṟu paṭutti, viyappumikka vāypāṭṭāl itu kūṟappaṭṭami kāṇka. pāliki - pāttiram, utaṭukaḷ; ivvāṟu itaṉkaṇ cilēṭiyum payiṉṟuḷḷatu.	(pāṭal 262 mā. a.)
amara eṉṉum uvamurupu -
‘vēy amar tōḷi’ (mūṅkil pōṉṟa tōḷkaḷi yuṭiyavaḷ) eṉṟa toṭaril amara eṉṉum uvamurupu mey uvamam paṟṟi vantatu; nūṟpāvil eṭuttōtap peṟāta urupukaḷuḷ oṉṟu  itu.	(to. po. 286 pērā.)
amivaṇi -
potuvāka ulakattārāl kūṟappaṭum kuṟi ciṟappāka nōkka niṟivākavē amivatākak kūṟum aṇi.
e-ṭu :	‘matiyiṉ kaḷaṅkam vaṉappuṟuttum mātar
vataṉat tilaka vaki’
cantiraṉiṭattuḷḷa kaḷaṅkam eṉṟu potuvākap paḻikkap paṭum kuṟi, matiyam pōṉṟa mātarvataṉattiṟkut tilakam aḻaku ceyvatu pōla matikku aḻaku ceykiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟum ippāṭaṟkaṇ, amivaṇi amintuḷḷatu.			(to. vi. 366)
amivu - kuṟiviyum kuṇamāka ākkik koḷḷutal.
ayam -
